Layout invariants

  • Each crate is a single-purpose library; mem-cli and mem-bench are the only binary-entry-point crates.
  • conformance/ uses only public API surface (mem-api); never reaches into crate internals.
  • fuzz/ has its own Cargo.toml workspace override; not included in default cargo test.
  • benchmarks/ crate depends only on mem-api + mem-core; no internal crate leakage.
  • docs/adr/ is authoritative for every design decision; code comments reference ADR numbers.
  • templates/config.toml.template is the single source of truth for all tunables; no magic defaults.

11. Core Schema and Core Types

11.1 Identity Types

Type Repr Semantics
MemoryId struct MemoryId(Ulid) Monotonic, time-sortable; globally unique; immutable after creation
EmbeddingId struct EmbeddingId(Ulid) References a stored embedding vector; stable across compaction
NsId struct NsId(Arc<str>) Namespace identifier; URL-safe alphanumeric + -_; max 128 bytes
SessionId struct SessionId(Ulid) Per-agent session; bound to a NsId at creation; immutable
  • MemoryId ordering: lexicographic on Ulid bytes = creation-time order; no two records share an id.
  • EmbeddingId survives compaction and snapshot; store maintains stable mapping EmbeddingId → offset.
  • NsId comparison: byte-exact; case-sensitive; no aliases; isolation invariant enforced at store layer.
Ulid layout (128 bits):
  [ 48-bit ms timestamp | 80-bit random ]
  → monotonic within 1 ms; collision prob < 2^-80 per ms per node

Serialization: all id types implement serde::{Serialize, Deserialize} as 26-char Crockford base32 strings. bincode secondary encoding for WAL (raw 16-byte). Stable across versions.

Ownership: MemoryId + EmbeddingId + SessionId are Copy; NsId is Clone via Arc.

11.2 MemoryKind

enum MemoryKind {
    Working,     // transient, session-scoped; evicted to long-term on session end
    Episodic,    // specific events/observations with timestamp provenance
    Semantic,    // distilled facts, summaries; no single-event provenance
    Procedural,  // skills, how-to patterns; typically low decay rate
}
  • Persistence: Working — evicted on session close unless explicitly promoted. Others — durable.
  • Decay rate defaults: Working > Episodic > Semantic > Procedural (configurable per namespace).
  • Index routing: all kinds indexed by recency + importance; Working excluded from cold-tier archival.
  • Serialization: stored as u8 tag (0–3); stable; new variants append-only (no reuse of numeric tag).

11.3 MemoryRecord

struct MemoryRecord {
    id:            MemoryId,          // immutable; set at creation
    kind:          MemoryKind,        // immutable after creation
    content:       String,            // UTF-8; max 64 KiB enforced at ingest
    embedding_ref: EmbeddingId,       // must reference valid embedding; invariant enforced
    salience:      Salience,          // mutable; updated by decay + access boost
    created_at:    Timestamp,         // monotonic; immutable after creation
    last_access:   Timestamp,         // mutable; updated on every recall hit
    access_count:  u32,               // mutable; saturates at u32::MAX
    provenance:    Provenance,        // immutable after creation
    links:         Vec<MemoryId>,     // mutable; appended by consolidation; no duplicates
    ttl:           Option<Duration>,  // mutable; None = no expiry; 0 = delete immediately
    namespace:     NsId,              // immutable; set at ingest; isolation boundary
}
  • Ownership: heap-allocated; passed as Arc<MemoryRecord> across subsystem boundaries to avoid copy.
  • Mutation semantics: salience, last_access, access_count, links, ttl are the only mutable fields. All mutations go through the store write path (WAL entry before in-memory update).
  • Concurrency semantics: Arc<RwLock<MemoryRecord>> within session; cross-session mutations serialized through store layer.
  • Persistence semantics: every field serialized to WAL on creation; mutable field changes emit a WAL delta record (field tag + new value). Full record in snapshot.
  • Serialization: serde + bincode for WAL; serde + JSON for external API responses. Field order stable; unknown fields ignored on read (forward compat).
  • Compatibility: adding optional fields is non-breaking; removing or renaming fields requires WAL format version bump + migration path.
  • Invariant: embedding_ref always resolves; checked at store open + compaction. Dangling ref = MemError::EmbeddingMissing.

11.4 Salience

struct Salience(f32);   // range [0.0, 1.0]; clamped on construction
impl Salience {
    fn new(v: f32) -> Self;                  // clamp to [0,1]
    fn decay(&self, half_life: Duration, elapsed: Duration) -> Self;  // exp decay
    fn boost(&self, delta: f32) -> Self;     // clamp after add
}
  • Salience(0.0) = candidate for eviction; Salience(1.0) = pinned (never auto-evicted).
  • NaN rejected at construction; MemError::InvalidSalience.
  • Decay formula: s' = s * 2^(−elapsed / half_life); half_life per-kind, per-namespace override.
  • Access boost: +0.1 per recall hit (configurable); decays on next background sweep.
  • Serialization: f32 little-endian in WAL; f32 in JSON ("salience": 0.72).

11.5 Embedding

struct Embedding {
    dim:    u16,            // fixed per namespace/provider; mismatch = MemError::DimMismatch
    data:   Box<[f32]>,     // length == dim; L2-normalized on storage
    model:  Arc<str>,       // embedding model id; used to detect provider change
}
struct EmbeddingRef {
    id:     EmbeddingId,
    dim:    u16,
    model:  Arc<str>,
}
  • Ownership: Box<[f32]> avoids Vec capacity overhead; Clone is explicit (expensive).
  • Normalization: stored L2-normalized; cosine similarity reduces to dot product.
  • Dim mismatch: querying with dim != index.dim returns MemError::DimMismatch immediately.
  • Model change: model string mismatch triggers index rebuild gate; old embeddings remain readable but excluded from ANN until re-embedded.
  • Serialization: f32 array little-endian in WAL/snapshot; base64-encoded in JSON API.
  • Helpers: fn cosine(a: &Embedding, b: &Embedding) -> f32; fn dot(a: &Embedding, b: &Embedding) -> f32.

11.6 Provenance

struct Provenance {
    source:       String,              // URI, agent-id, tool-id, or "user"
    session:      SessionId,           // originating session
    derived_from: Vec<MemoryId>,       // parent records (consolidation lineage)
    ingested_at:  Timestamp,           // wall-clock time of ingest
}
  • Immutable after creation; WAL-durably stored with record.
  • derived_from empty = original observation; non-empty = summarization/reflection product.
  • Used by audit/explain path: trace from consolidated memory back to source observations.
  • Serialization: serde bincode in WAL; JSON in API. derived_from max 256 entries enforced.

11.7 Query

struct Query {
    text:            Option<String>,          // raw text; tokenized for BM25
    embedding:       Option<Embedding>,       // pre-computed query embedding; if None, embed text
    k:               usize,                   // top-K results requested; max 1000
    kind_filter:     Option<Vec<MemoryKind>>, // None = all kinds
    recency_bias:    f32,                     // [0,1]; weight on recency index in RRF
    importance_bias: f32,                     // [0,1]; weight on importance index in RRF
    filters:         MetaFilter,              // arbitrary metadata predicate
    as_of:           Option<Timestamp>,       // time-travel: exclude created_at > as_of
    namespace:       NsId,                    // required; no cross-namespace queries
}

struct MetaFilter {
    tags:       Option<Vec<String>>,          // AND-all; tag set membership
    kind:       Option<MemoryKind>,           // redundant with kind_filter; kept for API ergonomics
    min_salience: Option<Salience>,           // pre-filter; applied before ANN
    custom:     Option<Box<dyn Fn(&MemoryRecord) -> bool + Send + Sync>>, // runtime predicate
}
  • k = 0 → MemError::InvalidQuery.
  • text and embedding both absent → MemError::InvalidQuery (must have at least one).
  • as_of enforces time-travel invariant: index scan restricted to created_at <= as_of.
  • custom predicate applied post-retrieval (cannot be pushed into index); execution sandboxed by timeout.
  • Serialization: Query is serde-serializable except custom field (skipped; must be re-injected on deserialize).

11.8 RetrievalResult and ScoreComponents

struct RetrievalResult {
    record:     Arc<MemoryRecord>,
    score:      f32,                  // final fused score after RRF + MMR; range [0,1]
    components: ScoreComponents,
    rank:       usize,                // 1-based final rank
}

struct ScoreComponents {
    vec:        f32,   // ANN cosine similarity contribution (normalized)
    bm25:       f32,   // BM25 score contribution (normalized)
    recency:    f32,   // recency index score contribution
    importance: f32,   // importance (salience) contribution
    rrf_score:  f32,   // raw RRF aggregate before MMR
}
  • Determinism invariant: fixed index state + Query → identical (record, rank) sequence; tie-break: score desc, then MemoryId asc.
  • components is debug/explain output; not used for further computation downstream.
  • Serialization: JSON for API; score rounded to 6 decimal places in JSON (deterministic repr).

11.9 ContextBudget and Working Memory Types

struct ContextBudget {
    max_tokens: u32,    // hard ceiling; context assembly MUST NOT exceed; error if overflow
    reserved:   u32,    // tokens reserved for system prompt + agent template; deducted first
}

struct ContextWindow {
    budget:   ContextBudget,
    entries:  Vec<ContextEntry>,    // ordered by priority; assembled into prompt
    used:     u32,                  // running token count; invariant: used <= budget.max_tokens - reserved
}

struct ContextEntry {
    record:   Arc<MemoryRecord>,
    tokens:   u32,                  // pre-computed token count for this record's content
    priority: f32,                  // derived from score + kind weight; higher = kept on overflow
}
  • Budget invariant: ContextWindow::push returns MemError::BudgetExceeded if adding would breach limit; caller must evict before retrying.
  • Overflow policy: evict lowest-priority Working memories → promote to Episodic long-term before dropping from context.
  • Token counting: byte heuristic (chars/4) as default; swappable via TokenCounter trait for tiktoken-compatible impl.
  • Serialization: ContextBudget is config-level (serde); ContextWindow is runtime-only, not persisted.

11.10 ConsolidationJob

struct ConsolidationJob {
    id:          Ulid,
    kind:        ConsolidationKind,
    namespace:   NsId,
    targets:     Vec<MemoryId>,      // records to consolidate; non-empty
    created_at:  Timestamp,
    priority:    u8,                 // 0=high (user-triggered), 255=low (background sweep)
    status:      JobStatus,
}

enum ConsolidationKind {
    Summarize,   // cluster → summarize → new Semantic record
    Reflect,     // higher-order pattern extraction → new Semantic/Procedural record
    Merge,       // near-dup dedup → canonical record; tombstone losers
    LinkUpdate,  // recompute MemoryRecord.links for target set
}

enum JobStatus {
    Queued,
    Running { started_at: Timestamp, worker_id: u32 },
    Done    { finished_at: Timestamp, produced: Vec<MemoryId> },
    Failed  { reason: String, retries: u8 },
}
  • Persisted in job queue (mem-store namespace __consolidation__); survives crash.
  • targets deduplicated and sorted on enqueue; idempotent re-enqueue (same target set → same job if Queued).
  • status transitions: Queued → Running → Done | Failed; Failed retried up to max_retries (default 3).
  • Concurrency: jobs for same namespace run sequentially; cross-namespace parallel allowed.

11.11 LlmIface Trait

trait LlmIface: Send + Sync {
    async fn summarize(&self, texts: &[&str], hint: &str) -> Result<String, MemError>;
    async fn extract_facts(&self, text: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>, MemError>;
    async fn embed(&self, texts: &[&str]) -> Result<Vec<Embedding>, MemError>;
    async fn rerank(&self, query: &str, docs: &[&str]) -> Result<Vec<f32>, MemError>;
}
  • embed batch size limited by provider; implementation must chunk internally if needed.
  • rerank returns score per doc in input order; length must equal docs.len() or MemError::RerankLenMismatch.
  • All methods are cancellation-safe (caller may drop future); no partial state written to store.
  • Rejected alternative: concrete OpenAI/HuggingFace types — violates pluggability; external ML boundary must stay abstract.

11.12 StorageBackend and IndexBackend Traits

trait StorageBackend: Send + Sync {
    fn put(&self, record: &MemoryRecord) -> Result<(), MemError>;
    fn get(&self, id: MemoryId) -> Result<Option<Arc<MemoryRecord>>, MemError>;
    fn delete(&self, id: MemoryId, reason: EvictionReason) -> Result<(), MemError>;
    fn scan(&self, ns: &NsId, cursor: Option<MemoryId>, limit: usize)
        -> Result<Vec<Arc<MemoryRecord>>, MemError>;
    fn flush(&self) -> Result<(), MemError>;   // fsync WAL + store
}

trait IndexBackend: Send + Sync {
    fn insert(&self, id: MemoryId, embedding: &Embedding) -> Result<(), MemError>;
    fn remove(&self, id: MemoryId) -> Result<(), MemError>;
    fn search(&self, query: &Embedding, k: usize, ef: usize)
        -> Result<Vec<(MemoryId, f32)>, MemError>;
    fn rebuild(&self, records: &[(MemoryId, Embedding)]) -> Result<(), MemError>;
}
  • delete logs EvictionReason before removal; no silent loss invariant enforced here.
  • flush is synchronous; callers on write path block until durable.
  • IndexBackend::rebuild is atomic swap: build new index, swap pointer, drop old. Serves reads from old until swap.
  • Both traits are object-safe; boxed as Box<dyn StorageBackend> in runtime config.

11.13 MemError

enum MemError {
    // Storage
    WalCorrupted     { path: PathBuf, offset: u64, detail: String },
    StoreFull        { namespace: NsId, bytes_free: u64 },
    RecordNotFound   { id: MemoryId },
    EmbeddingMissing { id: EmbeddingId },
    // Index
    DimMismatch      { expected: u16, got: u16 },
    IndexCorrupted   { kind: String, detail: String },
    // Ingest / Retrieval
    InvalidQuery     { reason: String },
    DeduplicateConflict { id: MemoryId },
    BudgetExceeded   { used: u32, max: u32 },
    // LLM interface
    LlmUnavailable   { provider: String, cause: String },
    LlmTimeout       { provider: String, elapsed_ms: u64 },
    RerankLenMismatch { expected: usize, got: usize },
    // System
    Io               { source: std::io::Error },
    InvalidSalience  { value: f32 },
    NamespaceNotFound { ns: NsId },
    NamespaceQuotaExceeded { ns: NsId, quota: u64, used: u64 },
    Serialization    { detail: String },
    InternalInvariantViolated { detail: String },
}
  • thiserror derive; all variants include enough context to triage without stack trace.
  • WalCorrupted and IndexCorrupted are non-retryable; surface to operator immediately.
  • LlmUnavailable and LlmTimeout are retryable; retrieval falls back to cached embeddings / text-only path.
  • InternalInvariantViolated is a programming error; triggers tracing::error! + optional abort in debug builds.
  • Serialization: all variants serialize to JSON for API error responses; numeric error code stable per variant.

11.14 Timestamp and Time Handling

struct Timestamp(i64);   // microseconds since Unix epoch; monotonic within process via Instant
  • Timestamp::now() uses std::time::SystemTime; clamped to monotonic sequence within store (last-seen max).
  • as_of time-travel: record.created_at > as_of → excluded from index search and result set.
  • Serialization: i64 microseconds in WAL; ISO 8601 string in JSON API.
  • Leap seconds: ignored (POSIX time); not a concern for memory record ordering.

11.15 Session and Namespace Runtime Handles

struct SessionHandle {
    id:        SessionId,
    namespace: NsId,
    opened_at: Timestamp,
    config:    SessionConfig,       // decay rates, budget overrides, llm iface binding
}

struct SessionConfig {
    budget:         ContextBudget,
    working_cap:    usize,          // max Working records in context at once (default 50)
    decay_override: Option<HashMap<MemoryKind, Duration>>,  // half-life per kind
    llm:            Arc<dyn LlmIface>,
}
  • SessionHandle is !Send; scoped to creating thread/task; Drop triggers working-memory flush to long-term.
  • SessionConfig is Clone; shared via Arc across pipeline stages within a session.
  • Namespace must exist before session creation; MemError::NamespaceNotFound otherwise.

11.16 EvictionReason and Audit Log

enum EvictionReason {
    TtlExpired    { ttl: Duration },
    DecayEviction { final_salience: Salience },
    UserForget    { session: SessionId },
    Compaction    { superseded_by: MemoryId },  // merge/dedup: loser record
    QuotaEviction { namespace: NsId },
}
  • Every StorageBackend::delete call must supply an EvictionReason; stored in eviction audit log (__evictions__ namespace).
  • No-silent-loss invariant: eviction log is WAL-durable; survives crash; queryable via mem-cli inspect --evictions.
  • Compaction variant links to the canonical record that superseded it; lineage preserved.

11.17 Type Compatibility and Versioning Policy

Concern Rule
WAL format version u16 at file header; reader must handle version <= current; unknown = error
Field addition Non-breaking; old readers skip unknown bincode fields (explicit skip tags)
Field removal Breaking; requires WAL format version bump + migration script in scripts/
Enum variant addition Non-breaking for MemoryKind / EvictionReason; append-only numeric tag
Trait method addition Breaking; bump crate major version; document in CHANGELOG
MemoryId uniqueness Guaranteed by Ulid; no migration needed across store versions
NsId rename Not supported; treated as new namespace; migration = copy + delete old
  • All load-bearing types derive Debug, Clone (where appropriate), PartialEq (for testing), serde::{Serialize, Deserialize}.
  • MemoryRecord, Embedding, Provenance do NOT derive Hash (f32 fields); explicit Eq/Hash impls prohibited.
  • Public API types in mem-core follow semver strictly; internal crate types may evolve freely between patch releases.

12. Phase 0 — Skeleton — crates, core types, in-memory store, remember()/recall() exact-match round-trip (no embeddings)

12.1 Objectives

  • Cargo workspace: 13 crates (mem-core, mem-store, mem-index, mem-embed, mem-ingest, mem-retrieve, mem-consolidate, mem-forget, mem-context, mem-api, mem-telemetry, mem-cli, mem-bench).
  • Stabilize every canonical spine type: MemoryId, MemoryRecord, MemoryKind, Salience, Embedding, Provenance, Query, RetrievalResult, ContextBudget, NsId, MemError.
  • In-memory RecordStore (HashMap-backed), correct CRUD, no persistence yet.
  • Wire remember() → mem-ingest (passthrough, no extraction/embed) → mem-store (in-memory), and recall() → mem-retrieve (exact-match/linear scan) → Vec<RetrievalResult>.
  • All cross-crate calls synchronous Result<_, MemError> this phase; async trait boundaries defined, not yet exercised. No file I/O, no embeddings, no background threads — round-trip correctness only.

12.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P15 (Monotonic Time and Sortable IDs — MemoryId ULID), P8 (Explicit Provenance — Provenance struct), P12 (Fail Fast on Invariant Violation — TryFrom Salience guard), I1 (Durability — placeholder, no-op in Phase 0), ADR-01 (Rust), ADR-02 (Cargo workspace), ADR-04 (bincode), S1 (ULID spec).

  • Workspace root Cargo.toml: [workspace] members=[...13 crates...], resolver="2", edition="2021", rust-version="1.78"; shared deps ulid="1", serde{derive}="1", thiserror="1", tracing="0.1".

mem-core — src/lib.rs submodules (types-only, no logic):

Module Contents
ids MemoryId(Ulid) — new(), as_bytes(), Ord by ULID monotonic order
kinds MemoryKind { Working, Episodic, Semantic, Procedural }
salience Salience(f32) clamped [0,1]; TryFrom<f32> → MemError::InvalidSalience on OOB
embedding EmbeddingId(Ulid); Embedding { data: Vec<f32>, dim }; dim-check invariant
provenance Provenance { source, session: Ulid, derived_from: Vec<MemoryId> }
record MemoryRecord { id, kind, content, embedding_ref: Option<EmbeddingId>, salience, created_at: u64(μs), last_access, access_count, provenance, links: Vec<MemoryId>, ttl: Option<Duration>, namespace: NsId }
query Query { text, embedding: Option<Embedding>, k, kind_filter, recency_bias, importance_bias, filters: MetaFilter, as_of: Option<u64> }
result RetrievalResult { record: Arc<MemoryRecord>, score, components: ScoreComponents{vec,bm25,recency,importance} }
budget ContextBudget { max_tokens, reserved }; NsId(u64)
errors MemError (thiserror): NotFound, DuplicateId, InvalidSalience, DimMismatch, StoreFull, Io, Encode, Decode, BudgetExceeded, Poisoned
  • mem-store (src/memory_store.rs): MemoryStore{ inner: RwLock<StoreInner> }; StoreInner{ records: HashMap<MemoryId,Arc<MemoryRecord>>, by_ns: HashMap<NsId,BTreeSet<MemoryId>> }; insert/get/remove/scan_ns(ns)->Vec<Arc<MemoryRecord>> (full ns scan). tracing::trace! on every mutation.
  • mem-ingest (src/ingest.rs): IngestPipeline{store}; capture(content, kind, salience, ns, provenance, ttl) -> Result<MemoryId,MemError> — builds MemoryRecord directly (no extraction/dedup/embed this phase), embedding_ref=None, created_at=now_micros().
  • mem-retrieve (src/retrieve.rs): Retriever{store}; retrieve(&Query)->Result<Vec<RetrievalResult>,MemError> — scans query.filters.ns, exact-text-match (content.contains(text), empty text = all); score=salience.0, importance=salience.0, other components 0; sort score desc then id asc (determinism); truncate to k.
  • mem-api (src/api.rs): MemoryApi{ingest, retriever}; remember(...), recall(query), forget(id,ns) (delegates to mem-store::remove).
  • mem-telemetry: no-op init_tracing() via tracing_subscriber::fmt.
  • mem-cli (main.rs): remember <text> / recall <text> subcommands, JSON output.
  • Test harness (mem-api/tests/integration.rs): test_round_trip, test_namespace_isolation, test_forget, test_k_limit, test_determinism (same state+query → same ordered set on repeat).

12.3 Deliverable

  • cargo build --workspace clean, zero clippy::all warnings; cargo test --workspace 100% pass.
  • mem-cli remember "hello world" prints inserted MemoryId; recall "hello" prints JSON record.
  • All canonical types serde-serializable; operator inspects store via CLI; tracing spans on remember/recall visible at RUST_LOG=trace.

12.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
cargo test --workspace All tests pass, zero failures
test_round_trip Content preserved byte-for-byte
test_namespace_isolation 0 cross-ns leakage

remember latency (in-memory, 10k records) p99 < 500 µs
recall latency (exact-match, 10k records) p99 < 2 ms
Heap overhead per 10k MemoryRecord < 100 MB

forget then recall Empty result, no panic

12.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture file Purpose Expected output
fixtures/p0/round_trip.json Single remember → recall; validates field preservation content matches, id is ULID, access_count == 0
fixtures/p0/ns_isolation.json Two namespaces, mixed inserts Recall ns-A: only ns-A records
fixtures/p0/k_truncation.json Insert 10, query k=4 Exactly 4 results, sorted desc by salience
fixtures/p0/forget.json Insert → forget → recall Empty []
fixtures/p0/determinism.json Same store snapshot + query Identical result JSON on 10 replay runs
fixtures/p0/malformed_salience.json salience: 1.5 MemError::InvalidSalience returned, store unchanged
fixtures/p0/empty_content.json content: "" Allowed; recall with text: "" returns it
fixtures/p0/duplicate_id.json Inject same MemoryId twice Second insert → MemError::DuplicateId

12.6 Pitfalls

  • Clock skew on created_at: NTP slew moves SystemTime::now() backward → non-monotonic MemoryId vs timestamp; fix: track last_assigned_millis, clamp forward; signal mem_id_clock_clamp_total>0.
  • RwLock write starvation: std::sync::RwLock prefers readers on Linux → writer (insert/remove) p99 spikes under read-heavy load; recover via parking_lot::RwLock (fair policy).
  • scan_ns full-table O(n): scans all records not ns-targeted; recall latency grows with total store size not ns size; fix: enforce by_ns index in retriever.
  • Arc<MemoryRecord> refcount churn: scan_ns clones one Arc per record per call (profiler-hot, not incorrect); mitigate by batching into one Vec and passing a slice.
  • MemoryId(Ulid) serde asymmetry: string in JSON, u128 in bincode — inconsistent fixture format; document the chosen encoding in mem-core/SERIALIZATION.md.
  • query.k == 0: silently returns empty vec (caller may misread as "no results"); enforce k >= 1 at the API boundary, else MemError::InvalidQuery.

12.7 Performance Concerns

  • Full-namespace scan is O(n): fine < 10k records (budget 2 ms p99), bottleneck above — profile with criterion before Phase 3 indices land.
  • HashMap pointer-chasing on heap MemoryRecord: dense Vec-backed store considered, deferred to Phase 1 (slotmap/mmap slab).
  • Single RwLock on StoreInner covers all namespaces (shard by NsId planned Phase 1); acceptable for Phase-0 single-threaded tests.
  • content: String cloned on insert — switch to Arc<str> if flamegraph shows malloc > 5% in capture.
  • Two-pass sort (score then id tie-break) — merge into one sort_unstable_by with a compound comparator.

12.8 Edge Cases

  • query.text Unicode grapheme clusters: str::contains is byte-based, partial match possible — acceptable pre-Phase-3 BM25 tokenization.
  • MemoryRecord.links forward-references not-yet-inserted IDs: allowed (no referential-integrity check yet; Phase 1 adds soft validation).
  • NsId(u64::MAX) as sentinel avoided: NsId(0) reserved as NsId::default().
  • ttl: Some(Duration::ZERO): not enforced this phase (mem-forget unwired); record persists until Phase 8/9 decay integration.
  • Concurrent remember+forget on same MemoryId: RwLock exclusion prevents partial state.
  • Query.k = usize::MAX: clamp to records.len() before allocating, avoiding capacity overflow.
  • content with embedded NUL bytes: valid String, allowed; JSON-escaping requirement noted in SERIALIZATION.md.

12.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
RwLock::write panic (poisoned) MemError::Poisoned returned to caller All subsequent ops fail Rebuild store from fixtures Restart process; Phase-1 WAL enables replay
HashMap memory exhaustion OOM kill Full process Pre-flight capacity check via store.len() guard MemError::StoreFull at configurable threshold
ULID clock regression Non-monotonic IDs Ordering invariant broken Forward-clamp + mem_id_clock_clamp_total metric Detect and alert; no data loss
serde_json decode panic MemError::Decode propagated Single request fails Boundary error type prevents unwind propagation Return error; log malformed payload
Duplicate MemoryId injection MemError::DuplicateId Single insert rejected Store unchanged Caller regenerates ULID (monotonic — collision astronomically unlikely)

12.10 Operational Notes

  • Tracing: tracing::info_span!("remember", ns=ns.0, kind=?kind) wraps capture; recall span includes query.k and text.len(). Export: RUST_LOG=mem_api=debug.
  • Metrics (Phase-0 stubs): mem_remember_total, mem_recall_total, mem_store_records_gauge — registered as tracing::event! counters; wired to Prometheus in mem-telemetry Phase 11.
  • No migration needed: Phase-0 store is fully in-memory; process restart = empty store. Fixtures serve as reproducibility baseline.
  • Rollback: N/A in-memory; any regression caught by fixture replay (cargo test).
  • Compatibility: All types have #[non_exhaustive] where variants may be added; callers must handle _ arms.
  • Debugging aid: MemoryStore::dump_json() -> String method for offline inspection via CLI mem-cli dump.

13. Phase 1 — Durable store — append-only WAL + record store, mmap, fsync policy, crash recovery, compaction; durability invariants

13.1 Objectives

  • Replace Phase-0 in-memory HashMap backend with a durable, crash-safe mem-store engine.
  • Deliver: append-only WAL (fsync-per-commit or group-commit policy), mmap-backed record store, crash recovery via WAL replay, compaction (rewrite live records, truncate WAL).
  • Durability invariant: remember() returning Ok survives process kill + restart; record store fully rebuildable from WAL replay.
  • No-silent-loss invariant: eviction/removal logged with reason before execution.
  • Constraints introduced: async tokio::fs I/O on WAL path; parking_lot::RwLock replaces std::sync::RwLock on StoreInner; compaction runs as tokio::task::spawn_blocking.
  • Phase-0 in-memory path retained as InMemoryBackend for unit tests and embedded use.

13.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P1 (Durability Before Acknowledgement — fsync per commit), P2 (Crash-Only/Replayability — WAL replay in recovery.rs), P12 (Fail Fast on Invariant Violation — CRC32c on every WAL frame), I1 (Durability — sync_data before ACK), I8 (WAL Append-Only — no in-place mutation), ADR-04 (bincode framing), ADR-10 (custom WAL), ADR-11 (memmap2 mmap), S6 (POSIX fsync/fdatasync), PB13 (Ousterhout & Rosenblum 1992 — LFS sequential write model).

mem-store/src/
  lib.rs — re-exports, feature flags ("durable","in-memory")
  wal.rs — WAL writer/reader | record_store.rs — mmap slab + index
  compaction.rs — compaction engine | recovery.rs — WAL replay, integrity check
  snapshot.rs — point-in-time export | backend.rs — StorageBackend trait
  • WAL (wal.rs): wal.bin = 16-byte header (magic,version,flags,seq) + framed entries [crc32:u32][len:u32][op:u8][payload], op∈{Insert=1,Remove=2,Update=3}. WalWriter{file,buf:BufWriter<File>,seq:AtomicU64}; append(op)->Result<u64> bincode-serializes, writes framed entry, sync_data() if FsyncPolicy::PerCommit. FsyncPolicy∈{PerCommit, GroupCommit{interval_ms}, Never(test)}; group-commit batches via tokio::time::interval + bounded(1024) channel, one fsync/interval. WalReader::iter() validates CRC32, yields WalEntry{seq,op}. Checkpoint(seq) entry written atomically pre-compaction-complete as truncation marker.
  • Record store (record_store.rs): records.bin mmap slab, 4 KB page-aligned slots [flags:u8][id:16B][len:u32][payload]; flags LIVE=0x01,DELETED=0x02,COMPACTED=0x04. RecordStore{mmap:MmapMut, index:DashMap<MemoryId,SlotOffset>, free_list:SegQueue<SlotOffset>}. insert: bincode-serialize, claim slot (free_list or append), write, set LIVE, update index. remove: set DELETED, push free_list (WAL entry already written by caller). get: index→SlotOffset→mmap deserialize. memmap2-backed; flush() after batch writes.
  • Crash recovery (recovery.rs): recover(wal_path, store_path) — open store, find last checkpoint seq, replay WAL from seq+1 to EOF (apply Insert/Remove not yet in store), validate CRC32 per entry, skip malformed tail (crash-boundary partial write), log via recovery_entries_replayed/recovery_entries_skipped.
  • Compaction (compaction.rs): trigger when deleted_ratio>0.3 OR WAL size > wal_max_bytes. Sequence: write+fsync BeginCompaction(seq) → copy all LIVE slots to records.tmp → write+fsync wal.tmp with single Checkpoint(seq) → atomic rename both → reopen mmap, rebuild index → emit compaction_duration_ms/compaction_bytes_reclaimed. New writes continue to wal.bin during compaction; recovery replays the post-checkpoint gap.
  • StorageBackend trait (backend.rs):
trait StorageBackend: Send + Sync {
    fn insert(&self, record: MemoryRecord) -> Result<(), MemError>;
    fn get(&self, id: MemoryId) -> Result<Option<Arc<MemoryRecord>>, MemError>;
    fn remove(&self, id: MemoryId) -> Result<(), MemError>;
    fn scan_ns(&self, ns: NsId) -> Result<Vec<Arc<MemoryRecord>>, MemError>;
    fn record_count(&self) -> usize;
}

DurableBackend (WAL+mmap) and InMemoryBackend (Phase-0 HashMap) both implement it.

  • Snapshot (snapshot.rs): export_snapshot(path) iterates LIVE records, writes NDJSON, returns SnapshotMeta{seq,record_count,sha256} — operator inspection + DR.
  • mem-store/tests/: test_wal_replay (kill-9-equivalent, reopen, all present), test_compaction (200→delete 100→compact→100 live), test_crc_corruption (byte-flip, 1 entry skipped+logged), test_fsync_per_commit (mock File call verification), test_concurrent_writes (16T×1000, 0 lost/0 panics).

13.3 Deliverable

  • cargo test -p mem-store 100% pass including crash-recovery and compaction tests.
  • mem-cli gains --durable --data-dir <path> flag; records survive process restart.
  • Operator can inspect wal.bin with mem-cli wal-dump --data-dir <path> (prints seq, op, id).
  • mem-cli snapshot --data-dir <path> exports all live records as NDJSON + SHA256 checksum.
  • Metrics emitted: wal_append_latency_us, compaction_duration_ms, recovery_entries_replayed, store_live_records.

13.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
WAL replay after SIGKILL All committed records recovered; 0 loss
Compaction correctness record_count_after == live_before; store reopens cleanly
CRC-corrupt WAL recovery Skips tail entries; logs count; no panic
insert p99 latency (fsync-per-commit, SSD) < 5 ms
insert p99 latency (group-commit 10 ms interval) < 1 ms steady-state
get p99 latency (mmap, warm page cache) < 200 µs
scan_ns 10k records < 10 ms p99
Compaction 100k records Completes < 30 s; WAL truncated to < 1 KB
test_concurrent_writes (16T × 1000) 0 lost, 0 duplicate IDs
deleted_ratio after compaction < 0.05

13.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected
fixtures/p1/fresh_open.bin Empty store; open + insert 1 record record_count = 1
fixtures/p1/crash_mid_write.wal WAL truncated mid-entry (simulated crash) Recovery skips partial; recovery_entries_skipped = 1
fixtures/p1/post_compaction.bin Records.bin after compaction of 200→100 live = 100, deleted_ratio < 0.05
fixtures/p1/corrupt_crc.wal CRC32 byte-flip on entry 5 of 10 Entries 1–4 replayed; 5–10 skipped with warning
fixtures/p1/checkpoint.wal WAL with valid Checkpoint marker Recovery starts from Checkpoint seq
fixtures/p1/large_content.bin Record with content = 1 MB string Stored and retrieved intact; slot spans pages
fixtures/p1/duplicate_remove.wal Remove op for already-deleted ID Idempotent; no error, no double free

13.6 Pitfalls

  • Partial fsync on power loss: sync_data() flushes pages but not the directory entry; records.bin can be absent after a rename-then-crash — fix: fsync the parent directory after atomic rename.
  • mmap + O_DIRECT aliasing: page cache and mmap aren't O_DIRECT-safe together; WAL uses BufWriter<File> only, record store uses mmap only (never both on one fd).
  • Compaction rename race: crash between records.tmp→records.bin and WAL truncation leaves both files; recovery detects records.tmp + missing Checkpoint and re-runs compaction from WAL.
  • DashMap iteration during compaction: concurrent inserts mutate mid-scan; take a full read guard across all shards for the compaction snapshot phase.
  • Unbounded WAL growth: low delete-rate workloads never cross deleted_ratio; add wal_max_bytes as a second compaction trigger (documented Phase-1 constraint).
  • BufWriter does not fsync on drop: panic mid-write leaves data unflushed — explicit flush()+sync_data() in WalWriter::commit(), Drop impl warns if unflushed.
  • mmap growth: MmapMut needs a known size at map time; pre-allocate 64 MB chunks, ftruncate+remap on overflow, track mmap_remap_total.

13.7 Performance Concerns

  • sync_data() on NVMe: p50≈0.1 ms, p99≈3 ms, p99.9≈15 ms; group-commit amortizes, per-commit fine at agent-typical <10 writes/s.
  • mmap TLB shootdowns under high write rate on multi-core: write-once slots (flags-only mutation) minimize dirty pages.
  • bincode ≈500 ns/record at 1 KB content; batch-serialize before acquiring the write lock, hold lock only for slot+index update.
  • free_list (SegQueue) is lock-free but cache-line-bounces under contention; per-thread free lists deferred to Phase 3.
  • Compaction runs via spawn_blocking, capped to 1 concurrent (Semaphore(1)); compaction_blocked_total if wait > 100 ms.
  • scan_ns is a sequential mmap scan (OS prefetch helps); spatial ordering by NsId deferred to the Phase-3 index.

13.8 Edge Cases

  • Writes during compaction: post-rename recovery replays WAL seq > checkpoint (test_write_during_compaction).
  • Empty WAL on first open: no checkpoint, record_count=0 — valid state.
  • WAL u64 sequence overflow: ~2.5M years at 10M ops/day — no mitigation needed, documented only.
  • Concurrent remove+get: get may still return the record between WAL write and flag update — acceptable soft-delete race; Phase 8/9 decay is the hard boundary.
  • DashMap index is unbounded: max_record_count guard in insert → MemError::StoreFull at threshold.
  • Record content > 4 KB slot: overflow-chain slots (flags|=OVERFLOW_CHAIN, chain head stores next offset); covered by large_content.bin.
  • Disk full mid-compaction: incomplete .tmp detected on next open, deleted, compaction re-run from WAL; compaction_aborted_total logged.

13.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
wal.bin corruption (hardware error) CRC mismatch on replay Records after corrupt entry unrecoverable recovery_entries_skipped alert Restore from snapshot; replay WAL from snapshot seq
records.bin mmap corruption get() returns garbled data or panics Affected record only Read validates CRC on deserialization Remove slot; replay WAL to re-insert
Compaction crash mid-rename records.tmp present on next open No data loss (WAL intact) Re-run compaction from full WAL Automatic on recovery
Disk full WalWriter::append → Io(ENOSPC) All remember() fail MemError::Io returned to API Operator alert; compaction to reclaim space
mmap remap failure (ENOMEM) MemError::Io on capacity growth All inserts fail Return error; existing data intact Reduce max_record_count; restart
DashMap poisoned shard Panic propagated Entire store inaccessible Catch via std::panic::catch_unwind in backend Restart; WAL replay

13.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: wal_append_latency_us, store_live_records, store_deleted_slots, compaction_duration_ms, compaction_bytes_reclaimed_total, recovery_entries_replayed_total, recovery_entries_skipped_total, mmap_remap_total.
  • Tracing: wal_append{seq,op}, compaction{trigger}, recovery{entries_replayed,entries_skipped} spans; recovery warnings at WARN, compaction start/complete at INFO, per-record ops at TRACE.
  • Deployment: --data-dir must be on a filesystem honoring fsync (no tmpfs in production).
  • Migration: Phase-0→1 has no in-place migration (Phase 0 was in-memory only); cold start with empty store.
  • Backup: mem-cli snapshot exports consistent NDJSON; recommended daily to S3/GCS, retain 7 days.
  • Schema versioning: WAL header version field; recovery rejects mismatch with MemError::VersionMismatch rather than risk silent corruption.

14. Phase 2 — Embeddings + chunking — embedding provider interface, chunker, embedding cache, vector storage layout

14.1 Objectives

  • Implement mem-embed: abstract EmbedProvider trait, text chunker, per-provider embedding cache (LRU + persistent), vector flat-file store layout.
  • Wire mem-ingest to call EmbedProvider::embed() for each chunk; store EmbeddingId in MemoryRecord.
  • Embedding-record consistency invariant: every live record with embedding_ref = Some(id) has a valid vector in the embedding store; embedding store never contains orphans (enforced by Phase-4 compaction linkage).
  • Constraints introduced: LlmIface::embed(texts: &[&str]) -> Result<Vec<Embedding>, MemError> is the only external dependency; mem-embed is provider-agnostic.
  • Chunker must handle multilingual text; chunk size configurable; overlap configurable.
  • Embedding cache: in-process LRU (content_hash → EmbeddingId) + optional persistent cache in embeddings.bin (flat float32 array); cache reduces re-embed cost on duplicate/near-duplicate content.

14.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P3 (Embedding–Record Atomicity — EmbeddingId paired with MemoryRecord), P5 (Bounded Resource Usage — LruEmbedCache bounded by max_entries), P9 (Backpressure Everywhere — batch embed rate-limits), I3 (Embedding–Record Consistency — dim_check invariant), ADR-08 (abstract LlmIface embed), ADR-09 (LlmIface trait), PB9 (Chen et al. 2020 — bi-encoder dot-product over cosine for L2-normalized vectors).

mem-embed/src/
  lib.rs — re-exports | provider.rs — EmbedProvider trait + LlmIface adapter
  chunker.rs — TextChunker (sentence + token boundary) | cache.rs — LruEmbedCache + PersistentEmbedStore
  store.rs — EmbeddingStore (flat file) | dim_check.rs — dimension consistency enforcement
  • EmbedProvider trait (provider.rs):
trait EmbedProvider: Send + Sync {
    fn embed_batch(&self, texts: &[&str]) -> Result<Vec<Embedding>, MemError>;
    fn dim(&self) -> usize;
    fn model_id(&self) -> &str;
}

LlmIfaceAdapter{iface:Arc<dyn LlmIface>,dim,model_id} implements it; contract: result length == texts.len(), each dim==self.dim() else MemError::DimMismatch. MockEmbedProvider: deterministic hash-based vectors, no external calls.

  • TextChunker (chunker.rs): ChunkerConfig{strategy:ChunkStrategy, chunk_tokens, overlap_tokens, tokenizer:TokenizerKind}; ChunkStrategy∈{FixedToken,SentenceBoundary,RecursiveSplit}; TokenizerKind∈{Whitespace,Bpe{vocab_path},CharCount}. chunk(text,cfg)->Vec<Chunk{text,start_byte,end_byte,index}>; sentence boundary splits on .?!\n\n Unicode-aware, never mid-word; overlap = last overlap_tokens of previous chunk carried forward. Invariant: full input byte-covered, no drops.
  • LruEmbedCache (cache.rs): {inner:Mutex<LruCache<ContentHash,EmbeddingId>>, capacity}; ContentHash=SHA256(text||model_id); lookup/insert; default capacity 50k entries (~3.2 MB); metrics embed_cache_{hits,misses,evictions}_total.
  • PersistentEmbedStore (store.rs): embeddings.bin header [magic:4B][version:u32][dim:u32][count:u64] + records [id:16B][norm:f32][vector:dim×f32]. EmbeddingStore{mmap:MmapMut, index:DashMap<EmbeddingId,u64_offset>, dim}; insert validates vec.dim==self.dim, appends, updates index; get = index→offset→mmap read. l2_norm co-stored for fast cosine (dot/(‖a‖‖b‖)). EmbeddingId↔MemoryId linked via MemoryRecord.embedding_ref only (reverse map/orphan detection deferred to Phase-4 compaction).
  • dim_check.rs: DimGuard{expected} singleton per store; first insert sets expected (via OnceLock), every later insert/open validated against it, else MemError::DimMismatch.
  • mem-ingest integration: IngestPipeline gains embed_provider, embed_cache, embed_store. capture(): chunk → per-chunk cache lookup → on miss batch-call embed_provider.embed_batch → store vector → insert MemoryRecord{embedding_ref:Some(id)}. Multi-chunk content: one record per chunk, shared provenance.derived_from root id (full summary lands Phase 7). Cache misses are collected and embedded in one batch call.
  • mem-embed/tests/: test_chunk_coverage, test_embed_cache_hit, test_dim_mismatch, test_persistent_store_reopen (1000 entries, reopen), test_ingest_embedding_ref, test_multi_chunk (N records + provenance link).

14.3 Deliverable

  • cargo test -p mem-embed 100% pass.
  • mem-api::remember() with real or mock EmbedProvider stores embedding_ref in every record.
  • embeddings.bin persists across restarts (same dim guard enforced on reopen).
  • mem-cli embed-info --data-dir <path> prints: dim, count, model_id, cache hit rate.
  • MockEmbedProvider available for all downstream tests (no real LLM calls required).

14.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
test_chunk_coverage 0 bytes dropped or duplicated beyond configured overlap
test_embed_cache_hit Cache hit rate 100% on repeated identical content
test_dim_mismatch MemError::DimMismatch returned; no panic
test_persistent_store_reopen All 1000 embeddings recoverable; dim preserved
test_ingest_embedding_ref embedding_ref = Some(id) on every inserted record
embed_batch latency (mock, 32 chunks) < 1 ms (no I/O)
embed_batch throughput (mock) > 10,000 chunks/s
Embedding store size for 100k × 1536-dim < 600 MB (≈ 100k × 1536 × 4 B + overhead)
Cache memory at 50k entries < 10 MB

14.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected
fixtures/p2/chunk_basic.txt 500-word ASCII text; FixedToken(128, overlap=16) N chunks; byte coverage verified
fixtures/p2/chunk_unicode.txt Mixed CJK + Latin; sentence boundary No mid-grapheme splits
fixtures/p2/chunk_empty.txt Empty string chunks = []
fixtures/p2/embed_cache_round_trip.json Insert text → EmbeddingId; repeat same text Same EmbeddingId returned
fixtures/p2/dim_mismatch.json Provider returns dim=512 but store dim=1536 MemError::DimMismatch
fixtures/p2/persistent_store.bin Pre-built 100-entry embedding store (dim=4, mock) All 100 retrievable on reopen
fixtures/p2/orphan_check.json Delete MemoryRecord but leave embedding Phase-4 compaction fixture reference; Phase-2 notes orphan detection deferred
fixtures/p2/overlap_content.json Chunks with overlap; verify overlap bytes match chunk[i].text[-overlap:] == chunk[i+1].text[:overlap]

14.6 Pitfalls

  • Dim-guard race on first insert: two concurrent inserts on an empty store could both pass a dim==0 check; fix with OnceLock<usize> so the first winner sets dim atomically.
  • Cache keyed on text only: provider switch without cache flush returns stale wrong-model vectors — key must be SHA256(text||model_id), plus a flush_model(model_id) API.
  • Overlap slicing on multibyte chars: byte-offset slicing can split a UTF-8 sequence — always slice at char_indices() boundaries.
  • embed_batch partial failure: mid-batch provider error with partial writes already applied — make the batch transactional (allocate IDs, call provider, write all-or-none).
  • embeddings.bin grows unbounded: Phase 2 does not compact it; orphaned vectors accumulate — track embed_store_orphan_bytes, compaction lands Phase 4.
  • Flat-file random access is cold: get(id) seeks per call; HNSW (Phase 3) loads all vectors into RAM at startup, making embeddings.bin cold-path only.
  • BPE tokenizer init cost (50–200 ms): cache the TextChunker instance, never reconstruct per record.

14.7 Performance Concerns

  • Real-provider embed_batch round-trip: p50≈50 ms, p99≈500 ms; batch ≤128 chunks/call, FuturesUnordered with ≤4 concurrent provider calls.
  • SHA-256 cache-key cost ≈500 ns/call at 1 KB text (hot path); consider FxHasher for the in-process LRU key, reserving SHA-256 for the persistent on-disk key.
  • embeddings.bin records must stay 4-byte aligned (16(id)+4(norm)+dim*4, padded).
  • Mutex<LruCache> contends under parallel ingest — shard by ContentHash % 16.
  • Vector footprint: 100k×1536-dim f32 ≈ 614 MB; 1M records ≈ 6 GB, requiring quantization (Phase-3 option).
  • Cold-start get() mmap page faults: madvise(MADV_SEQUENTIAL) during bulk load, MADV_RANDOM during retrieval.

14.8 Edge Cases

  • Single token exceeding chunk_tokens: emit one oversized chunk, log chunk_oversized_total.
  • Provider returns NaN/Inf: dim_check doesn't validate values — add validate_values flag to insert, scanning for NaN/Inf → MemError::InvalidEmbedding.
  • chunk_tokens=0 or overlap_tokens>=chunk_tokens: rejected by ChunkerConfig::validate() (MemError::InvalidConfig; overlap must be < chunk size to avoid an infinite loop).
  • Provider/model switch mid-session changes dim: DimGuard rejects; operator migrates (truncate+reload) or uses a separate NsId.
  • Concurrent embed + Phase-4 compaction on embeddings.bin: coordinate via Arc<RwLock<EmbeddingStore>>, compaction takes write lock after draining active inserts.
  • EmbeddingId (Ulid) collision: negligible (2^80 random bits), documented not mitigated.
  • Empty embed_batch(texts=[]): must return Ok(vec![]), not an error (test_empty_batch).

14.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
Provider unavailable MemError::Io / HTTP 503 remember() fails for new content Retry with exponential backoff (3× max); circuit-breaker after 5 consecutive failures Manual flush after provider restored
embeddings.bin corruption get() returns garbage / deserialization fails Affected embeddings unusable; ANN index stale embed_store_corruption_total metric; alert Re-embed affected records via mem-cli re-embed --ns <ns>
Dim mismatch on reopen MemError::DimMismatch at store open Store inaccessible DimGuard blocks open; no partial reads Operator migrates: export NDJSON snapshot, truncate, reload with correct dim
LRU cache OOM (misconfigured capacity) Process heap OOM Process kill Capacity cap enforced; StoreFull before OOM Reduce lru_capacity in config
Partial batch failure MemError mid-batch Affected records have embedding_ref = None Ingest marks records as embedding_pending; retry queue Re-drive pending records via background retry task (Phase-5 ingest pipeline)
embeddings.bin full (no compaction) MemError::Io(ENOSPC) New embeddings fail Alert on embed_store_size_bytes gauge Trigger manual compaction; Phase-4 automation

14.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: embed_cache_hit_rate, embed_cache_evictions_total, embed_batch_latency_ms (by provider), embed_store_record_count, embed_store_size_bytes, chunk_count_per_record, embed_orphan_bytes.
  • Tracing: embed_batch{provider,batch_size}, chunk{strategy,n_chunks}, cache_lookup{hit} spans.
  • Config keys (TOML): embed.{model_id,dim,cache_capacity,batch_size,max_concurrent_calls}; chunker.{strategy,chunk_tokens,overlap_tokens,tokenizer}.
  • Provider swap: changing embed.model_id makes embeddings.bin dim-incompatible — truncate/reload or new data-dir; mem-cli migrate-embeddings --new-model <id> re-embeds (Phase-11 tooling).
  • Persistent cache value: avoids redundant provider calls across restarts for stable long-running content; tune lru_capacity against embed_cache_hit_rate.
  • Security: embeddings.bin holds vector representations of all content — same access controls as records.bin; no plaintext content in the embedding file.
  • Testing: MockEmbedProvider used by all integration tests; CI requires no network access.

15. Phase 3 — Vector ANN index (HNSW) — graph build/insert, k-NN search, recall/latency tradeoffs, on-disk persistence of the graph

15.1 Objectives

  • Production-grade HNSW ANN index in mem-index, pluggable via AnnBackend trait. Targets: p99 k-NN <= 5 ms at 1M records (D=1536), recall@10 >= 0.95, insert throughput >= 500/s sustained.
  • Scope: layered graph construction, insert, search, delete-by-marking, on-disk persistence (snapshot+WAL delta), recovery, namespace isolation.
  • Constraints: no unsafe outside mem-store's mmap region; nodes keyed by MemoryId; dim fixed per namespace at creation; HnswIndex and any alternative backend (hnswlib/usearch FFI) satisfy the same AnnBackend trait.
  • Invariants: every inserted id has a live graph node; deletes are soft-tombstoned (excluded from results, reaped at compaction); graph rebuilds from WAL deltas on crash recovery.

15.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P4 (Retrieval Determinism — deterministic HNSW search + MemoryId tie-break), P5 (Bounded Resource Usage — ef_construction cap, tombstone ratio guard), I4 (Retrieval Determinism — identical ef_search → identical ranked set), I10 (Index-Store Record Count — HNSW node count == live record count), ADR-06 (built-in HNSW vs FAISS), PB3 (Malkov & Yashunin 2020 — HNSW layer probability, neighbor heuristic), R4 (faiss HNSW production reference).

Crate: mem-index, module hnsw.

mem-index/src/hnsw/
  mod.rs — HnswIndex, HnswConfig, layer graph structures
  node.rs — Node: neighbors per layer, soft-delete flag, MemoryId
  search.rs — greedy beam search (ef_search), layer entry-point descent
  insert.rs — select_neighbors (heuristic), connect bidirectional
  persist.rs — snapshot serialization (bincode/zstd), delta WAL entry
  backend.rs — AnnBackend trait
  • AnnBackend trait (backend.rs):
trait AnnBackend: Send + Sync {
    fn insert(&self, id: MemoryId, emb: &[f32]) -> Result<(), MemError>;
    fn delete(&self, id: MemoryId) -> Result<(), MemError>;
    fn search(&self, emb: &[f32], k: usize, ef: usize) -> Result<Vec<(MemoryId, f32)>, MemError>;
    fn len(&self) -> usize;
    fn dim(&self) -> usize;
    fn snapshot(&self, w: &mut dyn Write) -> Result<(), MemError>;
    fn restore(r: &mut dyn Read, cfg: HnswConfig) -> Result<Box<dyn AnnBackend>, MemError>;
}
  • HnswConfig: m=16 (neighbors/layer), m_max0=32 (layer-0 neighbors), ef_construction=200 (insert beam width), ml=1/ln(M) (level normalization), dim (fixed per namespace at create), distance=Cosine (dot-product on L2-normalized vectors).
  • Graph structure: Arc<RwLock<GraphInner>> wraps Vec<NodeSlot> (dense pool, internal u32 node_id); NodeSlot{mem_id, emb:Box<[f32]>, layers:Vec<SmallVec<[u32;M]>>, deleted:AtomicBool}; HashMap<MemoryId,u32> id→slot; entry point AtomicU32, max layer AtomicUsize.
  • Insert (Malkov & Yashunin §3): draw level l=floor(-ln(rand)*ml) → greedy-descend entry-point layers L..l+1 (ef=1) → at layers l..0 beam-search ef_construction candidates → select_neighbors_heuristic prunes to M (M_max0 at layer 0), adds bidirectional edges, evicts excess → append to pool, update entry point if l>max_layer → write-lock covers only the prune/connect/append step (beam search is read-locked) → emit WalEntry::Insert{mem_id,level,neighbors_per_layer}.
  • Search: read-locked descent layers L..1 (ef=1 greedy) → layer 0 priority-queue beam search at caller ef_search (default 64) → cosine distance on unit-norm vectors → skip deleted nodes from results (still traversed as intermediaries) → top-k (MemoryId,score) desc.
  • Persistence: snapshot = bincode+zstd(3) of GraphInner to {ns}/hnsw.snap (topology only — neighbor lists + level, embeddings stay in mem-store); delta WAL {ns}/hnsw.wal append-only [len][bytes] (insert/delete/set-ep); open = load snap + replay deltas; consolidation triggers snapshot + WAL truncation; WAL delta fsynced before remember() returns Ok.
  • Namespace isolation: one HnswIndex instance (pool + WAL) per NsId.
  • Observability: counters hnsw.{insert,delete,search}.total, hnsw.tombstone.count; histograms hnsw.{insert,search}.duration_ms, hnsw.search.ef_actual; gauges hnsw.node.count, hnsw.layer.max, hnsw.tombstone.ratio.
  • Tests: recall@10≥0.95 vs brute-force on 1000 random embeddings; crash-recovery (kill w/o snapshot, reopen, all searchable); soft-delete (200 insert/50 delete, deleted ids absent); namespace isolation (cross-ns query empty); dim mismatch → MemError::DimMismatch.

15.3 Deliverable

  • HnswIndex compiles, passes unit+property tests, implements AnnBackend; mem-cli index stats --ns <ns> prints node_count/tombstone_ratio/max_layer/snap_size (WAL byte size, snapshot age also inspectable).
  • mem-bench hnsw produces recall@10 and p50/p99 insert/search latency at 100K/500K/1M.

15.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Threshold
recall@10 at 1M records (D=1536, ef=64, cosine) >= 0.95
p99 search latency at 1M, ef=64 <= 5 ms
Insert throughput (single thread) >= 500/s
Crash recovery: all pre-crash inserts searchable 100%
Tombstoned records never appear in results 0 leaks in 10K searches post-delete
WAL delta fsync per insert verified via fsync counter metric
Dimension mismatch returns MemError::DimMismatch test passes
Snapshot + WAL replay round-trip: recall unchanged delta <= 0.001

15.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture file Purpose Expected output
fixtures/hnsw/insert_1k.bin 1000 unit-norm f32 vecs + gt k-NN recall@10 >= 0.95
fixtures/hnsw/crash_wal.bin Partial WAL (truncated mid-entry) Truncated entry ignored; prior entries replayed
fixtures/hnsw/dim_mismatch.req Insert D=128 into D=1536 index MemError::DimMismatch
fixtures/hnsw/delete_regression.bin 200 inserts, 50 deletes, search Deleted ids absent; recall on survivors >= 0.93
fixtures/hnsw/namespace_isolation.bin Two ns, same embeddings, cross-search Empty result set
fixtures/hnsw/snap_reload.bin Snapshot + 100 WAL deltas Identical ranked results pre/post reload
fixtures/hnsw/duplicate_id.req Insert same MemoryId twice Second insert is idempotent or MemError::Duplicate
fixtures/hnsw/malformed_inputs.req zero-length embedding, NaN/Inf, k=0, ef<k MemError::InvalidInput, no panic

15.6 Pitfalls

  1. Stale entry-point after delete: entry point soft-deleted → search returns empty/wrong; detect via hnsw.entry_point.deleted>0; recover by scanning layer-max nodes for the first live one, updating atomically.
  2. Write-lock contention under bulk insert: full-GraphInner write lock serializes inserts (bidirectional edges need mutability); shard by node-id range (16 shards, independent RwLock); watch hnsw.lock.wait_ms.
  3. WAL replay divergence on partial fsync: power loss mid-write leaves a partial tail; CRC/length mismatch detects it; truncate at last valid CRC boundary, log wal.truncated_bytes.
  4. Recall degradation at high tombstone ratio: deleted nodes still occupy neighbor slots, reducing effective M; recall@10 drops past ratio>20%; trigger compaction (rebuild excluding tombstones) at ratio>0.15.
  5. ef_construction vs. recall regression: lowering it for speed permanently starves affected nodes of shortcuts; enforce ef_construction >= 2*M at config validation.
  6. L2-norm underflow: an all-zero embedding (failed embed call) yields 0/0 NaN cosine distance, collapsing search; validate |emb|>1e-6 at insert, else MemError::InvalidInput.
  7. Snapshot size explosion: storing full embeddings in-snapshot would be ~6 GB at 1M×D=1536; snapshot topology only (neighbor lists+level), fetch vectors from mem-store on reload — target <200 MB at 1M nodes.

15.7 Performance Concerns

  • Cosine distance (D multiply-adds/candidate) dominates — SIMD or manual unroll for MSRV-stable; target <1 µs/distance at D=1536.
  • Dense-pool neighbor lists are non-contiguous (cache thrash) — sort by insertion order (natural for ULID keys); cold layers (L>2) use separate arena from hot layer-0.
  • BinaryHeap allocation per search — pre-allocate with_capacity(ef_search) in thread-local storage.
  • Read-lock across the whole beam search blocks concurrent inserts — epoch-snapshot the node pool at search start, release graph lock before the distance loop.
  • Bidirectional-edge writes touch O(M×L) neighbor lists per insert (64 at M=16,L=4) — stage per-layer updates, apply under lock in one pass.
  • Arc refcount churn under contention (no GC, but still tail latency) — crossbeam-epoch reclamation for the node pool.

15.8 Edge Cases

  • k > index.len(): return all live nodes ranked, no panic. Empty namespace: empty vec, not an error.
  • ef_search < k: clamp to max(ef_search,k) internally, emit a warning metric.
  • Concurrent insert+delete of the same MemoryId: AtomicBool deleted flag, last write wins, no data race.
  • WAL delta referencing a node absent from the snapshot (corrupted file): OOB slot index detected, invalid delta skipped, WAL flagged dirty, full rebuild required.
  • Node-pool exhaustion (u32 id space): guard returns MemError::IndexFull near u32::MAX.
  • Snapshot concurrent with insert: snapshot read-locks and drains GraphInner, inserts block for its duration (hnsw.snapshot.duration_ms).
  • Dimension-change attempt post-creation: MemError::DimMismatch (dimension immutable per namespace).

15.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
Graph corruption (bad edges) Recall@10 metric < 0.90 All queries in ns degraded Isolate ns; serve degraded Rebuild from mem-store embeddings
WAL loss (disk failure) wal.open_error counter All unsnapshot inserts lost Replay from mem-store WAL if cross-referenced mem-cli index rebuild --ns <ns>
Snapshot write failure hnsw.snapshot.error counter Next restart requires full WAL replay (slow) Continue serving; retry snap Operator alert; schedule retry
Entry-point corruption Empty search results + non-empty index All searches in ns Scan for live entry point Auto-heal in search path
OOM during bulk load OOM kill Process crash Load in batches (streaming insert API) Restart; resume from WAL

Postmortem always captures hnsw.tombstone.ratio, hnsw.node.count, wal.size_bytes at incident time — recall regression is the primary SLO signal.

15.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics (namespaced by ns): node_count, tombstone_ratio, max_layer, snap_size_bytes, insert/search duration histograms, ef_actual distribution, wal_size_bytes, snap_age_seconds.
  • Tracing: hnsw.insert{ns,level,duration}, hnsw.search{ns,k,ef,result_count,duration} spans.
  • CLI: mem-cli index {stats,rebuild,snapshot}.
  • Deployment: HnswConfig immutable post-creation; M/ef_construction change requires full rebuild; config stored in namespace metadata, validated on open.
  • Migration: new DistanceFn variants (e.g. Euclidean) require rebuild; cosine is the only Phase-3 default, others behind a feature flag.
  • Rollback: snapshot+WAL are self-describing (version header); forward-incompatible changes require explicit migration.
  • Alert thresholds: tombstone_ratio>0.15 → compaction; snap_age>1h → snapshot; sampled recall<0.92 → page on-call.

16. Phase 4 — Keyword + hybrid retrieval — inverted index/BM25, score fusion (RRF), recency + importance scoring components

16.1 Objectives

  • BM25 inverted index (mem-index) over MemoryRecord.content; recency scorer (exponential decay on created_at/last_access) and importance scorer (Salience scaled by access_count).
  • HybridRetriever (mem-retrieve) fuses vector-ANN + BM25 + recency + importance via Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF), then an MMR diversity pass.
  • Constraints: BM25 in-memory with WAL persistence (HNSW pattern); RRF k=60 configurable; recency/importance bias weights come from Query; retrieval-determinism invariant holds.
  • Targets: BM25 p99 <=2 ms at 500K records; hybrid fusion p99 <=10 ms; MRR@10 >=0.85 on synthetic benchmark.

16.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P4 (Retrieval Determinism — RRF k=60 stable sort + MemoryId tie-break), P5 (Bounded Resource Usage — BM25 posting list size cap), I4 (Retrieval Determinism — identical ranked output on identical state), ADR-06 (ANN lane via AnnBackend), ADR-07 (BM25 hand-rolled), PB4 (Cormack et al. 2009 — RRF formula, k=60 robustness), PB5 (Carbonell & Goldstein 1998 — MMR λ diversity), PB8 (Robertson & Zaragoza 2009 — BM25+ parameters k1/b), PB1 (Park et al. 2023 — recency×importance×relevance weights).

mem-index/src/bm25/mod.rs — BM25Index/Config, term→posting list | tokenizer.rs — tokenize+stopword filter
mem-index/src/bm25/posting.rs — sorted Vec<(doc_id,tf,field_len)> | persist.rs — snapshot+WAL delta
mem-index/src/{recency,importance}.rs — RecencyScorer/ImportanceScorer
mem-retrieve/src/hybrid.rs — HybridRetriever | rrf.rs — rrf_fuse() | mmr.rs — mmr_rerank()
mem-retrieve/src/rerank.rs — RerankHook trait (LlmIface placeholder) | mod.rs — recall() entry
  • BM25Index: term dict HashMap<String,u32>+Vec<PostingList>; DocMeta{mem_id,field_len,deleted} per doc_id, bidirectional MemoryId↔doc_id map. Score Σ_t IDF(t)·tf(k1+1)/(tf+k1(1-b+b·dl/avgdl)), k1=1.2,b=0.75 (BM25Config); IDF ln((N-df+0.5)/(df+0.5)+1) incremental; avgdl running mean updated on insert/delete; soft-delete via DocMeta.deleted, reaped at compaction.
  • Tokenizer: split [^a-zA-Z0-9], lowercase, stopword filter (default English 50 words, no stemming — pluggable TokenFilter); empty content → empty vec, not error.
  • Insert path (post store-write): tokenize → compute field_len → update avgdl → append (doc_id,tf) per unique term → WAL BM25WalEntry::Insert{doc_id,mem_id,tokens} → fsync before return.
  • BM25 search: tokenize query → accumulate per-doc score across term posting lists (skip deleted) → sort desc → top-k.
  • RecencyScorer: score=2^(-(now-ts)/half_life); ts=last_access if newer else created_at; default half-life 24h (working/episodic), 7d (semantic/procedural).
  • ImportanceScorer: score=salience·(1+access_weight·ln(1+access_count)), access_weight=0.1 default, clamped [0,1].
  • HybridRetriever.recall(query): ANN search (k*4) → BM25 search (k*4) → compute recency/importance over candidates → rrf_fuse([L_vec,L_bm25,L_rec·recency_bias,L_imp·importance_bias], k=60) → top k*2 → MMR rerank (λ default 0.5) → top-k → optional RerankHook (LlmIface) → populate RetrievalResult.components{vec,bm25,recency,importance}. as_of filter (created_at<=as_of) applied before all steps.
  • RRF: rrf_score(d)=Σ_i 1/(k+rank_i(d)), k=60. MMR: λ·relevance-(1-λ)·max_sim(selected), embeddings fetched from mem-embed cache.
  • Sync: BM25Index in Arc<RwLock<BM25Inner>>; HybridRetriever holds independent Arc refs per sub-index, no cross-lock ordering.
  • Observability: counters bm25.{insert,search}.total, retrieve.hybrid.total; histograms bm25.search.duration_ms, retrieve.hybrid.duration_ms, rrf.candidate_count, mmr.diversity_score; span retrieve.recall{k,kind_filter,recency_bias,importance_bias,result_count}.
  • Tests: BM25 exact-term top-3; RRF fusion vs known ground truth; MMR selects diverse over near-dupes; hybrid determinism (same query twice); recency/importance bias=1.0 tie-break; as_of time-travel exclusion.

16.3 Deliverable

  • BM25Index/RecencyScorer/ImportanceScorer (mem-index) + HybridRetriever (mem-retrieve) compile, pass tests; mem-api::recall() routes through HybridRetriever; RetrievalResult.components populated and observable.
  • mem-bench hybrid reports MRR@10/NDCG@10/p99 latency at 100K/500K; mem-cli retrieve --query .. --k 10 --show-scores prints ranked results with component scores.

16.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Threshold
BM25 p99 query latency at 500K records <= 2 ms
Hybrid fusion p99 latency at 500K records <= 10 ms
MRR@10 on synthetic benchmark (500 queries) >= 0.85
RRF determinism: same query 100x -> identical ranked set 100%
as_of time-travel: future records excluded 100% in fixture
MMR: diversity (1 - avg pairwise cosine) >= threshold >= 0.3
BM25 WAL crash recovery: all pre-crash terms searchable 100%
component scores populated in every RetrievalResult 0 missing in 1K queries

16.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected output
fixtures/bm25/insert_500.jsonl 500 records with known term distribution Top-3 for target query contains gold record
fixtures/bm25/crash_wal_bm25.bin Truncated WAL at entry boundary Truncated entry dropped; prior entries valid
fixtures/hybrid/rrf_fusion.json 3 ranked lists + ground truth Exact fusion rank match
fixtures/hybrid/mmr_diversity.json 10 near-dupes + 3 diverse candidates Diverse results in top-5
fixtures/hybrid/as_of_query.json Records at T1, T2; query as_of=T1 T2 records absent
fixtures/hybrid/recency_bias.json Identical relevance, varying timestamps Most-recent ranks first at bias=1.0
fixtures/hybrid/importance_bias.json Identical relevance, varying salience Highest salience ranks first at bias=1.0
fixtures/bm25/empty_query.req Empty query string Empty result, no panic
fixtures/bm25/all_stopwords.req Query is only stop words Empty result, no panic

16.6 Pitfalls

  1. Posting list unsorted after bulk insert: parallel out-of-order appends break scoring — sort during compaction, debug_assert sorted invariant at search time.
  2. avgdl drift on delete: un-subtracted field_len inflates avgdl, shrinking BM25 scores over time — subtract at delete (DocMeta retains field_len); watch bm25.avgdl gauge trend.
  3. RRF k=0 divides by zero — enforce k>=1 in BM25Config validation (default 60).
  4. MMR embedding fetch latency: 200 candidate random reads from mem-store — pre-fetch in batch via the mem-embed cache before the MMR pass.
  5. Stop-word language mismatch: non-English content passes English stopword filter unaffected, inflating foreign-term IDF — stopword list configurable per namespace (documented limitation), watch bm25.unique_term_count.
  6. Recency clock skew: backward SystemTime::now() jump inflates recency for old records — use Instant delta from index open for relative scoring, absolute timestamps only for as_of.
  7. Stale kind_filter: applied post-fusion wastes ANN/BM25 work on irrelevant kinds — push filter into both sub-queries (BM25 checks DocMeta.kind; ANN metadata filter lands Phase 6).

16.7 Performance Concerns

  • Posting-list iteration is cache-unfriendly for high-frequency terms — gap-encoding/SIMD-PFOR compression for lists >1000 entries deferred to Phase 6; uncompressed Vec for now.
  • RwLock<BM25Inner> blocks search during insert — split into immutable Arc-shared BM25Snapshot + small pending-delta buffer, swap under write-lock.
  • Candidate-set explosion (k*4 ANN + k*4 BM25 + all recency/importance) — cap pool at min(k*8, 2000) before RRF.
  • MMR O(n²) similarity at n=200 (40K ops, negligible) — precompute the pairwise matrix once; cap MMR input at 200.
  • BM25 IDF recompute is O(vocab) per insert — mark dirty per-term, recompute lazily at search time.
  • Per-query Vec<String> tokenization allocation — SmallVec<[&str;32]> over a scratch buffer.

16.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty query text: all sub-queries empty, HybridRetriever returns empty vec (not an error). All records deleted: same empty-result behavior.
  • k > live records: return all, ranked, no panic.
  • recency_bias=0.0 and importance_bias=0.0: RRF fuses only L_vec+L_bm25, valid.
  • Concurrent query during BM25 insert: snapshot pattern gives bounded-staleness consistent reads.
  • as_of=0 (epoch): empty result, not an error.
  • half_life=Duration::ZERO: guarded to >=1s in config, else 0.0 (no NaN).
  • mmr_lambda=0.0: pure-diversity selection, valid use case.
  • RRF rank up to k*8=2000: 1/(60+2000) is f32-safe, no overflow.

16.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
BM25 index corruption Nonsense scores; avgdl wrong Keyword queries in ns degraded Disable BM25, vector-only fallback Rebuild BM25 from mem-store
RRF wrong order MRR@10 telemetry drop Retrieval quality degraded Log component scores, diagnose bias weights Adjust RRF k, re-test
MMR embedding fetch fails mem-store read error + counter Diversity pass skipped Graceful degrade to RRF top-k Retry next query; investigate store
Hybrid query timeout >50 ms Timeout metric Single query Circuit breaker returns partial results Profile slow sub-query; tune ef_search/BM25 cap
BM25 WAL corruption Checksum mismatch on replay ns BM25 unavailable Vector-only fallback Rebuild BM25 WAL from record store

Postmortem signals: retrieve.hybrid.duration_ms p99, bm25.avgdl trend, mmr.diversity_score distribution, per-query component breakdown.

16.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics (ns-labeled): bm25.{insert.total,search.duration_ms,doc_count,term_count,avgdl}, retrieve.hybrid.duration_ms, rrf.candidate_pool_size, mmr.diversity_score.
  • Tracing: retrieve.recall span with children ann.search, bm25.search, rrf.fuse, mmr.rerank (duration + result count each).
  • Deployment: BM25Config{k1,b,stop_words} in namespace metadata (rebuild on change); HybridRetriever bias weights and RRF k are runtime-configurable, no rebuild.
  • Rollback: BM25 snapshot version-tagged, prior format supported 1 major version.
  • Alerts: sampled MRR@10<0.80 → page; avgdl growing without inserts → check delete path; hybrid p99>20 ms → check ef_search/BM25 cap.
  • Disable BM25: bm25_weight:0.0 for embed-only namespaces (e.g. procedural memory).

17. Phase 5 — Memory kinds + schema — working/episodic/semantic/procedural, provenance, links, write routing by kind

17.1 Objectives

  • Fully instantiate MemoryKind semantics: write routing, retention, index participation, consolidation eligibility per kind; Provenance tracking (source/session/derived_from) with referential integrity; bidirectional links graph (forward+reverse index) in mem-index; NsId-scoped namespace management (create/delete/config, per-ns kind policies).
  • Constraints: MemoryRecord schema fixed since Phase 0, no migration needed. Link graph is soft-referential — dangling links (target evicted) are tolerated and logged, not fatal.
  • Targets: remember() with full provenance <2 ms overhead vs baseline; link traversal depth=2 <5 ms at 100K records.

17.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P7 (Namespace Isolation — NsId scopes all operations), P8 (Explicit Provenance — derived_from chain validated), P10 (Separation of Concerns — KindPolicy decoupled from retrieval logic), I7 (Namespace Isolation — cross-ns queries must fail), ADR-12 (RwLock for link index concurrency), PB7 (Gutierrez et al. 2024 — HippoRAG link-graph personalized PageRank inspiration).

Crates affected: mem-core (kind policy constants), mem-ingest (routing), mem-index (link graph), mem-store (namespace metadata), mem-api (namespace ops), mem-context (working memory caps), mem-forget (kind-aware TTL+decay).

mem-core/src/kind.rs — KindPolicy{retention,ttl_default,index_flags,consolidation_eligible,context_cap}
mem-core/src/provenance.rs — Provenance, ProvenanceBuilder, derived_from validation
mem-index/src/links.rs — LinkIndex: forward+reverse maps, add_link/remove_link/traverse
mem-store/src/namespace.rs — NsMetadata, NsRegistry, create_ns/delete_ns/list_ns
mem-ingest/src/router.rs — WriteRouter: dispatch to kind-specific pipeline stages
mem-api/src/namespace.rs — ns_create()/ns_delete()/ns_list()/ns_config()

KindPolicy table:

Kind Default TTL Indices Consolidation Context cap Eviction
Working 1h vec+BM25+recency+imp No (promoted by mem-context) 80% budget First (→episodic)
Episodic 30d vec+BM25+recency+imp Yes (summarize→Semantic) 15% budget Recency+salience
Semantic None vec+BM25+imp (no recency) Yes (reflect/merge) 5% budget Salience only
Procedural None vec+BM25+imp (no recency) No 0% (explicit inject) Never auto-evict

index_flags bitmask {VEC=1,BM25=2,RECENCY=4,IMPORTANCE=8} controls per-insert index participation; consolidation_eligible feeds mem-consolidate's job queue; context_cap is the ContextBudget.max_tokens fraction reserved per kind (mem-context).

  • WriteRouter.route(record)->RoutePlan{embed,index_flags,ttl}: Working/Episodic get all indices (ttl 1h/30d); Semantic/Procedural get BM25+vec+importance only, ttl=None, Procedural additionally skips recency.
  • Provenance{source,session:SessionId,derived_from:Vec<MemoryId>}: derived_from validated at write time — each id must exist in store or same batch, else MemError::MissingProvenance(id) (strict) or warn+continue (lenient, per-namespace config). ProvenanceBuilder ensures session always set.
  • LinkIndex: forward+reverse HashMap<MemoryId,HashSet<MemoryId>>; add_link/remove_links (idempotent); traverse(id,depth) BFS, deduplicated. Persisted via links.wal (AddLink/RemoveLink deltas) + links.snap bincode snapshot. Dangling target (not in mem-store) increments link.dangling and is skipped. Populated from record.links+provenance.derived_from (mem-ingest) and reflection links (mem-consolidate).
  • NsRegistry: HashMap<NsId,NsMetadata> backed by ns_registry.json; NsMetadata{ns_id,name,created_at,kind_policies,hnsw_dim,bm25_config,provenance_mode}. create_ns→NsId, delete_ns soft-deletes (marks for eviction), list_ns. All indices/WALs/snapshots prefixed {ns_id}/; cross-ns queries rejected at the API layer.
  • Query.kind_filter: Option<Vec<MemoryKind>>: BM25/ANN skip excluded kinds via DocMeta/node metadata.
  • Write path: route() → apply KindPolicy TTL if record.ttl=None → write to mem-store → index per index_flags → LinkIndex.add_link for links+derived_from entries → emit ingest.kind.{kind} counter.
  • Observability: counters ingest.{working,episodic,semantic,procedural}.total; gauge store.kind.{kind}.count; link.dangling.count; histogram ingest.route.duration_us.
  • Tests: per-kind routing correctness (index membership); TTL enforcement (mock clock + mem-forget eviction); provenance strict-mode rejection; link traversal (chain A→B→C→D→E, traverse(A,2)={B,C}); namespace isolation; kind_filter; ns delete isolates records.

17.3 Deliverable

  • Kind-aware routing active for all four kinds; Provenance populated+validated on every remember(); LinkIndex.traverse() via CLI+mem-api; namespace CRUD operational, all indices ns-isolated; mem-cli ns create/delete/list/stats work.
  • mem-bench kinds reports per-kind insert/query latency and depth-2 link traversal at 100K.

17.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Threshold
Per-kind index routing correctness 100% in routing unit tests
Provenance strict mode: missing parent rejected 100% of invalid inputs
Link traversal depth=2 at 100K records <= 5 ms p99
Working memory TTL default = 1h enforced 100% in TTL fixture
Procedural records absent from recency index 0 false positives in 1K queries
Namespace isolation: no cross-ns leakage 0 cross-ns results in isolation test
remember() provenance overhead vs. no-provenance <= 2 ms additional latency
Kind filter in recall() returns only matching kinds 100% in kind-filter fixture

17.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected output
fixtures/kinds/routing_all_kinds.jsonl One record/kind, index membership check Each kind in exactly its expected index set
fixtures/kinds/ttl_working.json Working record + mock clock advance Evicted after TTL
fixtures/kinds/provenance_strict.json derived_from with non-existent id MemError::MissingProvenance
fixtures/kinds/provenance_lenient.json Same, lenient mode Warning logged, record stored
fixtures/kinds/link_chain_5.json A→B→C→D→E links traverse(A,2)={B,C}; traverse(A,4)={B,C,D,E}
fixtures/kinds/dangling_link.json Link to evicted record dangling counter +1, traverse skips it
fixtures/kinds/ns_isolation.json ns1 insert + ns2 query Empty result from ns2
fixtures/kinds/kind_filter_query.json Mixed kinds, filter=[Semantic] Only semantic returned
fixtures/kinds/procedural_recency.json Procedural record + recency query Absent from recency-sorted results
fixtures/kinds/malformed_kind.req Unknown kind string / unknown NsId MemError::InvalidInput / MemError::UnknownNamespace

17.6 Pitfalls

  1. Stale kind label post-promotion: working→episodic promotion updates the record but not DocMeta.kind — make promotion atomic (store update + DocMeta.kind + re-index + remove from working recency index).
  2. Unbounded link graph: consolidation reflection links grow forward-map entries without pruning — cap links.len() at 50, prune weakest (lowest target salience) on overflow.
  3. derived_from self-reference (circular): validate at write time, reject with MemError::CircularProvenance.
  4. Namespace delete races with in-flight writes: state machine {Active,Deleting,Deleted}; remember() checks state under read-lock, MemError::NamespaceDeleted if not Active.
  5. KindPolicy override not retroactive: policy changes are forward-only — existing records keep old TTL (documented in NsMetadata).
  6. Procedural records auto-evicted by mistake: mem-forget must special-case eviction_priority=Never and skip Procedural entirely.
  7. Link WAL replay ordering: WAL entries carry a monotonic seq so RemoveLink never replays before its AddLink for the same pair.

17.7 Performance Concerns

  • LinkIndex HashMap rehash under bulk insert — pre-size via NsMetadata.expected_record_count hint (default 10K).
  • Provenance validation is one store read per derived_from entry — batch all parent-id lookups into one round-trip.
  • route() is hot-path — branch-free [KindPolicy;4] array indexed by MemoryKind as usize.
  • NsRegistry lookup per write — cache Arc<NsMetadata> per NsId in a DashMap, refreshed only on ns_config() mutation.
  • BFS traversal allocates a visited set per call — thread-local scratch HashSet, clear() between calls.
  • Per-kind/per-ns atomic counters at high write rate — relaxed ordering, aggregate at telemetry flush (1s).

17.8 Edge Cases

  • Unknown NsId in recall(): MemError::UnknownNamespace, never falls through to a default.
  • record.ttl=None on Working: WriteRouter applies KindPolicy.ttl_default (None means "use policy default").
  • Empty links: valid no-op. Duplicate ids in derived_from: deduplicated at write time.
  • Zero-record namespace: all queries empty, ns_stats returns zero counts, no error.
