  • kind_filter=Some([]): treated as no filter (documented semantic).
  • Duplicate namespace names: allowed (NsId/Ulid is the unique key), operator warned.
  • kind field is immutable post-insert; no re-routing on direct mutation.
  • Crash mid-link-WAL-write: replay truncates at last valid CRC, dropped links logged (link.recovery.dropped_count).

17.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
Link index WAL corruption link.open_error+dropped_count Links missing, traversal incomplete Serve without them, warn Rebuild from MemoryRecord.links
Namespace accidentally deleted ns.deleted event log ns records inaccessible Soft-delete, 24h grace period mem-cli ns restore --ns <id> in window
Procedural auto-evicted (policy bug) forget.eviction.kind.procedural>0 Procedural memories lost Fix policy; no auto-recovery Restore from archive
Provenance strict-mode false positives ingest.provenance_error.total elevated Writes rejected unnecessarily Switch to lenient via ns_config Investigate refs, re-submit
Link index OOM OOM kill Traversal unavailable Reduce link cap, evict oldest entries Restart, lower link.max_per_record

Postmortem signals: store.kind.{kind}.count trend, link.dangling.count, ingest.provenance_error.total, ns.state.

17.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics (ns+kind labeled): store.kind.{kind}.count, ingest.{kind}.total, link.{add,remove}.total, link.dangling.count, link.traverse.duration_ms, ns.count, ns.state.
  • Tracing: ingest.route{kind,ttl,index_flags}, link.traverse{depth,result_count}, ns.create/ns.delete spans.
  • CLI: mem-cli ns {create,delete,list,stats}, mem-cli links traverse --id <id> --depth 2.
  • Deployment: KindPolicy defaults baked in; ns-level overrides in NsMetadata take precedence, no migration needed; new MemoryKind variant is a breaking schema change (WAL version bump + migration tool).
  • Rollback: NsMetadata versioned, v1 supported indefinitely via a deserialization shim.
  • Alerts: link.dangling.count>100/min → investigate eviction policy; strict-mode provenance errors >0 → alert; unbounded store.kind.working.count → check mem-context/mem-forget eviction wiring.

18. Phase 6 — Ingest / write path — fact + entity extraction via LLM interface, dedup, conflict resolution (update vs append)

18.1 Objectives

  • mem-ingest: full write path from raw observation to durable MemoryRecord in store + all indices. Wire LlmIface::{extract_facts,extract_entities,embed}.
  • Dedup: content-hash + near-duplicate vector check (cosine >= threshold) before write. Conflict resolution: overlapping entity refs → Update/Append/Merge (consolidation queue).
  • Observation buffer: batching window for high-frequency ingest with back-pressure. Every record carries Provenance{source,session,derived_from}.
  • All writes honor the durability invariant (WAL fsync before Ok). Scope: mem-ingest + thin shims into mem-store/mem-index/mem-embed; LlmIface remains abstract.

18.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P1 (Durability Before Acknowledgement — WAL fsync before observe() Ok), P3 (Embedding–Record Atomicity — embed before insert, rollback on failure), P8 (Explicit Provenance — Provenance.derived_from chain), P9 (Backpressure Everywhere — ObservationBuffer bounded ring buffer), P11 (No Silent Loss — dedup rejects = WAL tombstone), I2 (No-Silent-Loss — explicit reason code for every rejection), I3 (Embedding–Record Consistency — embedding_ref written atomically with record), ADR-08 (abstract embed), ADR-09 (LlmIface extract), PB11 (Auer et al. 2007 — entity extraction schema), PB12 (Graefe 1994 — pipeline iterator backpressure).

mem-ingest/src/
  lib.rs — re-exports | pipeline.rs — IngestPipeline, observe() entry point
  extractor.rs — LlmIface dispatch (facts/entities) | chunker.rs — overlap-window chunker
  dedup.rs — DedupEngine (bloom+SHA256+vector) | conflict.rs — ConflictResolver decision table
  buffer.rs — ObservationBuffer (ring, batching, backpressure) | error.rs — IngestError→MemError

IngestPipeline{store,index,embed,llm:Arc<dyn LlmIface>,dedup,conflict,buffer,config,metrics}.

  • observe(obs)->Result<Vec<MemoryId>,IngestError>: push to ObservationBuffer (flush on batch-window close) → chunk (chunk_size/overlap) → per chunk: dedup.check_hash(sha256) skip-if-dup → parallel llm.extract_facts/extract_entities (bounded semaphore llm_concurrency) → embed_provider.embed() → dedup.check_vector(threshold) skip-if-near-dup, else index the embedding → build MemoryRecord → conflict.resolve()→{Append,Update(id),EnqueueMerge} → store.write_record (WAL fsync per FsyncPolicy) → index.insert_all (vec/BM25/recency/importance/metadata) → emit metrics → return ids.
  • DedupEngine: bloom filter (O(1) presence, FP rate <=1e-4) + SHA-256 HashMap<[u8;32],MemoryId> ring (exact) + near-dup vector check against HNSW (near_dup_threshold default 0.97 cosine, read-locked).
  • ConflictResolver: no existing entity record → Append; same source+session → Update(id); different source/session, sim>=0.90 → EnqueueMerge; contradictory predicate (LlmIface.detect_contradiction) → Update + derived_from chain; additive/non-overlapping → Append + links.
  • ObservationBuffer: bounded tokio::mpsc (capacity 256 default), tokio::time::interval batch-window flush, back-pressure (blocking or drop+metric per config), flush result via oneshot.
  • IngestConfig: chunk_size,overlap,near_dup_threshold,llm_concurrency,buffer_capacity,batch_window_ms,fsync_policy,max_facts_per_chunk.
  • Observability: root span ingest.observe{obs_id,kind,ns}, child spans per stage (extract_facts,embed_chunk,dedup_check,conflict_resolve,store_write,index_insert); counters ingest_records_total{kind,ns}, ingest_dedup_skipped_total, ingest_conflict_update_total, ingest_queue_depth; histogram ingest_e2e_latency_ms (P50/95/99 targets 50/150/400 ms).
  • Tests: roundtrip (observe→recall), dedup exact/near, conflict update/append, back-pressure (buffer fill), crash-recovery (kill mid-ingest, replay WAL, verify count).

18.3 Deliverable

  • mem-ingest compiles against mem-store/mem-index/mem-embed/mem-core; observe() returns Vec<MemoryId>; dedup (exact+near) and conflict routing (Update/Append/Merge) functional; all records WAL-durable before Ok.
  • Metrics visible via mem-telemetry (queue depth, e2e latency, dedup/conflict rates); mem-cli ingest stats --ns <ns> inspects counts.

18.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
cargo test -p mem-ingest 100% pass
Ingest e2e latency P95 (local LlmIface stub) <= 150 ms
Ingest e2e latency P99 <= 400 ms
Dedup exact recall 100% (no false negatives)
Dedup false-positive rate <= 1e-4
Conflict update correctness 100% on fixture set
WAL durability: crash-inject mid-write, replay -> no loss 100% of records before crash present
Back-pressure: 10k obs burst, buffer=256 -> no panic, metric incremented pass
Memory usage during 100k record ingest <= 512 MB RSS
LlmIface concurrency: 8 parallel extractions, no deadlock pass (10-run soak)

18.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/ingest/basic_ingest.json Single observation, episodic 1 MemoryRecord in store
fixtures/ingest/dedup_exact.json Same text twice 1 record, dedup_hits=1
fixtures/ingest/dedup_near.json Paraphrase pair, cosine 0.98 1 record
fixtures/ingest/conflict_update.json Entity + same-session re-mention Update action, derived_from chain
fixtures/ingest/conflict_append.json Two additive facts, different entities 2 records, linked
fixtures/ingest/malformed_utf8.bin Invalid UTF-8 bytes IngestError::InvalidContent, no partial write
fixtures/ingest/empty_obs.json Zero-length content IngestError::EmptyContent
fixtures/ingest/crash_replay/ 50 obs, kill after 25, replay 25 records durable
fixtures/ingest/oversized_chunk.json 1 MB content Chunked correctly, sub-records linked
fixtures/ingest/concurrent_ingest.json 32 concurrent same-entity obs Dedup/conflict correct, no duplicates

18.6 Pitfalls

  • LlmIface timeout cascade: slow extract_facts exhausts the concurrency semaphore, stalling ingest (queue depth spikes) — per-call timeout (10s default) + circuit-breaker on repeated failures.
  • Bloom filter saturation: ~10M inserts push FP rate above threshold, silently dropping valid records — watch dedup_bloom_fp_rate, rotate filter, log rejects for audit.
  • Embed-before-dedup ordering: embedding before the cheap hash check wastes LlmIface calls on exact dupes — hash dedup first, then vector near-dup, embed only survivors.
  • ConflictResolver race: concurrent observe() for the same entity can both resolve to Append — serialize the resolve+store step with an entity-keyed DashMap<EntityId,Mutex<()>>.
  • FsyncPolicy::Never misconfigured in prod: silent data loss on crash — reject Never unless an explicit allow_loss=true flag is set; alert on wal_fsync_skipped_total>0.
  • Near-dup check vs stale HNSW snapshot: post-restart hash ring empty but HNSW loaded from snapshot → asymmetric dedup — tag both with an epoch, verify match on reload.
  • Chunker splits entity boundaries: spurious duplicate partial-entity extractions — sentence-boundary-aware chunker with overlap.

18.7 Performance Concerns

  • LlmIface calls (10–100 ms, RPC-class) dominate — saturate the concurrency semaphore, run extract+embed in parallel per chunk.
  • Large bloom filter (>=1 MB) causes L3 misses — one cache-aligned allocation; shard the hash ring (dashmap).
  • BM25 write-lock contention under high ingest rate — batch inserts, one lock acquisition per flush.
  • Per-embed() Vec<f32> allocation (6 KB at D=1536) at 1k rec/s = 6 MB/s pressure — pool buffers per EmbedProvider.
  • Blocking-send back-pressure serializes callers — try_send + explicit back-pressure metric, let callers retry/shed.
  • SHA-256 on full 1 MB chunks — cheap rolling hash (xxhash3) first pass, SHA-256 only to confirm near-matches.
  • FsyncPolicy::PerRecord serializes throughput — PerBatch trades N-records-at-risk for throughput.

18.8 Edge Cases

  • Concurrent ingest+compaction: compaction_lock (ingest holds read, compactor needs write) prevents overlap.
  • Zero facts extracted: valid — record stored with empty facts, kind=Episodic (observation value independent of extraction).
  • Embed dim mismatch (provider D=768 vs index D=1536): checked at startup, fails fast with ConfigError::DimMismatch.
  • Future created_at: rejected IngestError::FutureTimestamp (monotonic invariant at ingest entry).
  • Unknown NsId: IngestError::NamespaceNotFound, no partial write.
  • Rapid repeated Update conflicts: derived_from chain capped at depth 32, oldest truncated with a warning.
  • All-whitespace content post-normalization: zero chunks → IngestError::EmptyContent before any LlmIface call.
  • Mid-batch crash (20 buffered, 10 WAL-committed): only the 10 committed survive — consistent with the durability invariant; buffer state is transient by design.

18.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Containment Recovery
LlmIface extract timeout ingest_llm_timeout_total spike Pipeline slot stalls Circuit-breaker skips extract after N timeouts, stores raw observation Drain breaker after 60s, re-queue affected
Dedup bloom saturation dedup_bloom_fp_rate>1e-3 Silent loss of valid content Alert + rotate filter Audit rejected-log, manual re-ingest
store.write_record fails (disk full) store_write_error_total Ingest fully blocked IngestError::StoreFull, back-pressure Evict/archive old records, extend storage
Index insert fails post store-write index_insert_error_total Stored but unretrievable NEEDS_REINDEX flag, background job Reindex sweep scans WAL for the flag
Concurrent entity-lock deadlock Mutex timeout Two ingest tasks stall Timeout + exponential backoff (max 3) Alert if entity_lock_timeout>10/min
WAL replay produces duplicate IDs wal_replay_dup_id_total Corrupt index state Idempotent replay (skip known ids) Full re-index from WAL post-replay

Postmortem: mem-cli wal audit --ns <ns> --since <ts> enumerates committed vs lost, compared against the upstream observation log.

18.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: ingest_records_total, ingest_e2e_latency_ms, ingest_dedup_skipped_total, ingest_conflict_update_total, ingest_queue_depth, ingest_llm_timeout_total, ingest_bloom_fp_rate.
  • Tracing: root span ingest.observe{ns,kind,session}, per-stage children, LlmIface calls tagged llm.call_ms.
  • Logging: INFO per observe() entry/exit; WARN on bloom rotation/conflict escalation; ERROR on store-write failure; never log raw content — content hash only.
  • Config hot-reload: near_dup_threshold/llm_concurrency/batch_window_ms reloadable via SIGHUP (Arc<RwLock<IngestConfig>>).
  • Migration: new MemoryKind variant needs a WAL schema bump; incompatible versions rejected with IngestError::SchemaMismatch.
  • Rollback: older binary refuses to open a forward-incompatible WAL_SCHEMA_VERSION; operator snapshots + migrates.
  • CLI: mem-cli ingest stats --ns <ns> — record count, dedup hit rate, conflict breakdown, queue depth, recent e2e P95.

19. Phase 7 — Working memory + context assembly — token budgeting, packing, eviction working->long-term, prompt-context assembly

19.1 Objectives

  • mem-context: working memory store + context-window assembler. Working memory = short-lived MemoryKind::Working records in a per-session ring buffer, capacity-bounded by ContextBudget.max_tokens.
  • Token budgeting: per-record token count (tiktoken-compatible estimator), enforce max_tokens - reserved ceiling. Packing: rank working+retrieved records by salience × recency_decay × importance_bias, pack greedily.
  • Eviction: over-budget working records evict to long-term via mem-ingest (never silently dropped). Assembly produces PromptContext{system_prefix, memories:Vec<FormattedMemory>, token_count}.
  • Context-budget invariant: assembled context never exceeds max_tokens; overflow evicts to long-term first. Scope: mem-context reads from mem-retrieve, writes evictions via mem-ingest, no direct LLM calls (token estimator only).

19.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P5 (Bounded Resource Usage — ContextBudget.max_tokens hard cap), P9 (Backpressure Everywhere — eviction to long-term before budget overflow), P11 (No Silent Loss — evicted records go to mem-ingest, not discarded), I5 (Context-Budget — assembled context ≤ max_tokens at all times), ADR-03 (Tokio async session lifecycle), ADR-12 (RwLock on SessionRegistry), PB2 (Packer et al. 2023 — MemGPT paging semantics and ContextBudget.reserved), PB10 (Izacard et al. 2021 — FiD passage encoding order informs context packing priority).

mem-context/src/
  lib.rs — re-exports | working.rs — WorkingMemory: per-session ring buffer, insert/evict/list
  assembler.rs — ContextAssembler: pack(), token accounting, priority scoring
  budget.rs — ContextBudget, TokenEstimator trait, TiktokenEstimator
  formatter.rs — FormattedMemory, PromptContextFormatter (text/JSON/XML)
  eviction.rs — EvictionPolicy: priority queue, evict_to_longterm()
  session.rs — SessionRegistry: NsId×SessionId → WorkingMemory lifecycle

WorkingMemory{session,ns,records:VecDeque<WorkingRecord>,token_total,budget,metrics}; WorkingRecord{id,content,token_count,salience,created_at,last_access}.

  • insert(record): estimate token_count → if token_total+token_count > max_tokens-reserved, run eviction loop (pop lowest-priority = salience×recency_weight from a min-heap, evict_to_longterm delegates to mem-ingest::observe(kind=Episodic), decrement total) until space frees → push to VecDeque, increment total → emit working_mem_token_total gauge / working_mem_eviction_total counter.
  • ContextAssembler.assemble(query, retrieved): collect working+retrieved records → score priority = salience·recency_decay(age,halflife)·(1+importance_bias·importance), recency_decay=exp(-ln2·age/halflife) → sort desc → greedy-pack while running+token_count <= max_tokens-reserved-system_prefix_tokens → build PromptContext → assert token_count<=max_tokens (panic debug / ContextError::BudgetExceeded release) → emit context_assembled_token_count histogram.
  • TokenEstimator trait: count(text)/count_batch(texts); impls TiktokenBpe (tiktoken-rs) and CharDiv4Estimator (fallback).
  • EvictionPolicy: min-heap by priority; evict_to_longterm → IngestPipeline::observe(Observation{content,kind:Episodic,provenance:{derived_from:[id]}}); appends to per-session working_eviction_log (daily rotation) and the session's eviction_manifest (audit).
  • SessionRegistry: DashMap<(NsId,SessionId),Arc<Mutex<WorkingMemory>>>; get_or_create, close_session (evicts all remaining to long-term then removes); idle sessions auto-closed past session_ttl by a background sweeper.
  • Formatter: [MEMORY {kind} {created_at}]: {content} template; PromptFormat::{PlainText,Xml,Json}; system_prefix injectable per namespace.
  • Tests: budget-exact (no eviction), budget-overflow (evicts lowest-priority), eviction lands in mem-store, assembled order matches priority, estimator-vs-tiktoken accuracy (100 strings), session isolation, close_session evicts all, recency-decay ordering (older ranks lower at equal salience).

19.3 Deliverable

  • mem-context compiles against mem-core/mem-ingest/mem-retrieve; WorkingMemory::insert() auto-evicts to long-term on overflow; ContextAssembler::assemble() guarantees within-budget PromptContext; SessionRegistry manages multi-session/multi-namespace state.
  • Metrics (token utilization, eviction rate, assembly latency, token-count histogram); mem-cli context stats --session <id> shows usage/eviction/top-priority records.

19.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
cargo test -p mem-context 100% pass
Context budget invariant: assembled token_count <= max_tokens 100% of 10k random assemblies
Eviction correctness: evicted records in long-term store 100%
Priority ordering: assembled records monotonically non-increasing priority 100%
Assembly latency P95 (1000 candidates, budget 4096 tokens) <= 5 ms
Token estimator accuracy vs tiktoken within 2% for 95% of strings
Session isolation: 100 concurrent sessions, no cross-contamination pass
close_session evicts all working records 100%
Memory per session (1000 working records) <= 10 MB
Eviction log written for every evicted record 100%

19.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/context/budget_exact.json Fill to max_tokens exactly 0 evictions, token_count=max_tokens
fixtures/context/budget_overflow_single.json One record over budget 1 eviction, lowest-priority record gone
fixtures/context/budget_overflow_cascade.json 10x overflow Multiple evictions, priority order preserved
fixtures/context/eviction_to_longterm.json Evict + verify in store Evicted MemoryId present in mem-store
fixtures/context/session_isolation.json Two sessions, same ns Records do not cross
fixtures/context/assemble_priority.json Mixed salience/recency Output ordered by priority descending
fixtures/context/empty_working_mem.json assemble() with no working records PromptContext with only retrieved records
fixtures/context/malformed_budget.json max_tokens=0 ContextError::InvalidBudget
fixtures/context/session_close.json Close session, 20 working records All 20 in long-term store after close
fixtures/context/recency_decay.json Same salience, different ages Older records ranked lower

19.6 Pitfalls

  • Estimator drift from model: char/4 under-counts CJK/emoji, exceeding the real model limit — use tiktoken-rs BPE, inject model ID at init, fail fast if unknown.
  • Eviction blocks assembly: evict_to_longterm can block on LlmIface inside insert() — make it async with timeout, mark EVICTION_PENDING and drain in background on timeout.
  • Priority tie-break instability: equal-score records order non-deterministically — secondary sort by MemoryId (monotonic ULID).
  • SessionRegistry leak: crashed agents never close sessions — background sweeper force-closes idle sessions past session_ttl (default 1h).
  • Working memory is transient by design: lost on restart (only evicted-to-longterm records survive) — document explicitly, do not assume otherwise.
  • reserved=0 misconfiguration: leaves no room for the agent's own response — enforce reserved>=256 at startup.
  • Single oversized record > entire budget: insert() returns ContextError::RecordExceedsBudget, routes directly to long-term instead.

19.7 Performance Concerns

  • Priority sort is O(N log N) per assemble (~13ms at N=10k) — maintain a partial-sort BinaryHeap, re-sort only on new insertions.
  • Per-record tiktoken call (~10µs) adds ~10ms at N=1000 — cache token_count in WorkingRecord at insert, never recount.
  • SessionRegistry DashMap shard contention under multi-session throughput — shard by NsId first if contention >5% in profiling.
  • VecDeque+BinaryHeap dual structure kept in sync on insert/evict (O(log N) each).
  • Batch-insert eviction cascade: collect all to-evict, single observe_batch() call rather than N sequential calls.
  • Formatter string allocation per record — pre-allocate String::with_capacity(max_tokens*4).

19.8 Edge Cases

  • Budget shrink mid-session (hot-reload): next insert detects token_total > new_budget, eviction loop drains excess, no crash.
  • Concurrent insert+assemble on one session: Mutex<WorkingMemory> serializes; eviction runs via spawn_blocking so the mutex isn't held across the async call.
  • Zero-content record: token_count=0, never budget-evicted (only by session close or forget()).
  • Clock skew: SystemTime backward jump clamps recency-decay age to 0 (no negative age).
  • Record evicted between assembly and agent use: assembly is a snapshot, no consistency guarantee afterward (documented).
  • assemble() with no records at all: valid PromptContext{memories:[],token_count:system_prefix_tokens}.

19.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Containment Recovery
Budget invariant violated context_budget_exceeded_total Oversized context reaches model Debug panic / release ContextError::BudgetExceeded, empty context Recalibrate estimator/reserved
evict_to_longterm fails (store full) context_eviction_fail_total Working record silently at risk Log EVICTION_FAILED+content hash, alert Re-ingest from eviction failure log
SessionRegistry OOM RSS over threshold All sessions affected Sweeper force-closes LRU sessions Lower session_ttl, add RSS-based emergency eviction
Token estimator unavailable Startup check fails No assembly possible Fail fast at startup Supply valid tiktoken model ID, rebuild
Session not found on assemble context_session_not_found_total Empty context returned WARN log, empty PromptContext Operator recreates session

19.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: working_mem_token_total{ns,session}, working_mem_records_total, working_mem_eviction_total, context_assembly_latency_ms, context_assembled_tokens, context_budget_exceeded_total.
  • Tracing: context.assemble{session,ns,candidate_count,packed_count,token_count} with children score_candidates, greedy_pack, format_context.
  • Eviction log: eviction_log_{ns}_{session}_{date}.jsonl, one line/eviction (ts,id,content_hash,reason,token_count), daily rotation, 7-day retention.
  • Config hot-reload: max_tokens/reserved/recency_halflife via SIGHUP.
  • Deployment: stateless except SessionRegistry; zero-downtime deploy drains active assemble() calls (5s timeout) then force-evicts remaining working records.
  • CLI: mem-cli context stats --session <id> — token usage, record count, eviction rate, top-5 by priority.

20. Phase 8 — Consolidation / reflection — background summarization, reflection (higher-order memories), merge/dedup, link graph

20.1 Objectives

  • mem-consolidate: background pipeline for summarization, reflection, merge/dedup, link-graph maintenance. Summarize: cluster episodic records (temporal+topic), LlmIface::summarize()→new Semantic record. Reflect: periodic higher-order insights via LlmIface::reflect()→Semantic record tagged reflection=true.
  • Merge/dedup: detect near-duplicate Semantic records, merge to canonical, update links, retire duplicates (soft-delete). Link graph: bidirectional links maintained + traversable.
  • Non-blocking to ingest/retrieve (dedicated background tasks); no silent deletion — retired records marked status=RETIRED, audit trail preserved. Scope: mem-consolidate delegates store writes to mem-store, LLM calls to LlmIface, embedding to mem-embed.

20.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P2 (Crash-Only/Replayability — ConsolidationJob WAL-first, idempotent replay), P10 (Separation of Concerns — consolidation engine decoupled from ingest/retrieve), P11 (No Silent Loss — retired records marked RETIRED not deleted), P12 (Fail Fast on Invariant Violation — double-summarize guard via derived_from dedup), I12 (Consolidation Idempotency — replay of any job produces same result), ADR-03 (Tokio background tasks for engine), ADR-09 (LlmIface reflect/summarize), PB6 (Sarthi et al. 2024 — RAPTOR hierarchical cluster-summarize), PB1 (Park et al. 2023 — reflection as higher-order memory creation), PB7 (Gutierrez et al. 2024 — link-graph bidirectional edge maintenance).

mem-consolidate/src/
  lib.rs — re-exports | engine.rs — ConsolidationEngine: task loop, dispatch, worker pool
  summarizer.rs — cluster→summarize→store | reflector.rs — recent memories→reflect→store
  merger.rs — near-dup Semantics→merge→retire | link_graph.rs — bidirectional edges + traversal
  scheduler.rs — trigger policies (count/time/manual) | job.rs — ConsolidationJob enum+status

ConsolidationEngine{store,index,embed,llm:Arc<dyn LlmIface>,link_graph:Arc<RwLock<LinkGraph>>,scheduler,worker_pool:Arc<Semaphore>,metrics}. ConsolidationJob∈{Summarize{ns,record_ids}, Reflect{ns,since}, Merge{ns,candidate_pairs}, RelinkGraph{ns}}.

  • SummarizationPipeline: fetch records → agglomerative cluster by temporal gap + cosine similarity (O(N²), N<=50/job) → per cluster llm.summarize()→embed→build Semantic record (derived_from+links=cluster ids) → store.write_record (WAL-durable) → index → mark each source Status::Retired{derived_into:summary_id} (soft-delete) → link_graph.add_edges(summary,cluster,Summarizes) → emit consolidate_summarize_total/records_retired_total.
  • ReflectionPipeline: fetch recent Semantic records since job.since (window-capped) → top-reflection_sample_size by salience → llm.reflect()→Vec<ReflectionInsight> → per insight: embed, build Semantic record (salience 0.85, reflection=true, derived_from=sampled_ids) → store+index → link via ReflectedFrom → emit consolidate_reflections_total.
  • MergePipeline: per candidate pair fetch both → llm.merge()→MergeResult{merged_content,keep} or Distinct → if merged: store canonical (derived_from=[a,b]), retire both, link_graph.add_edges(canonical,[a,b],MergedFrom), remove retired from indices (not WAL/store) → emit consolidate_merge_total.
  • Candidate detection: every new Semantic record triggers an HNSW top-K scan; cosine > merge_sim_threshold (0.92 default) enqueues a Merge job, deduped against the merge manifest.
  • LinkGraph{forward,backward:HashMap<MemoryId,Vec<(MemoryId,EdgeKind)>>}, EdgeKind∈{Summarizes,ReflectedFrom,MergedFrom,UserDefined}; persisted link_graph.bin snapshot + WAL delta; add_edge atomic both-directions under write lock; neighbors(id,depth) BFS; traverse(id,filter) filtered.
  • Scheduler triggers: count-based (>summarize_threshold=50 new records → Summarize), time-based (reflect_interval=1h → Reflect), similarity scan (new Semantic → merge candidate scan), manual (API/mem-cli consolidate run).
  • Worker pool: Semaphore(max_concurrent_jobs=4), one permit held per job lifetime.
  • Tests: cluster→1 summary+10 retired; summary derived_from completeness; reflect produces >=1 Semantic; near-dup merge→1 canonical+2 retired; link-graph multi-hop traversal; count-trigger at 51 records; retired excluded from recall; crash mid-summarize replay shows no partial summary.

20.3 Deliverable

  • mem-consolidate compiles, runs via ConsolidationEngine::start(); summarize/reflect/merge pipelines produce correctly-linked records; LinkGraph persisted+traversable+consistent; scheduler fires on all trigger types.
  • Metrics (job rate, records retired, merge/reflection rate, worker utilization); mem-cli consolidate status --ns <ns> shows queue depth, last run times, status counts.

20.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
cargo test -p mem-consolidate 100% pass
Summarize: source records status=RETIRED after job 100%
Summarize: summary record derived_from contains all source IDs 100%
Reflect: produces >= 1 Semantic record per 100 episodic records verified on fixture
Merge: 2 near-dup Semantics (cosine 0.95) -> 1 canonical, 2 retired 100%
Retired records not returned by recall() 100%
LinkGraph persistence: restart + verify traversal 100% match
Consolidation job latency P95 (50 records, LlmIface stub) <= 500 ms
Worker pool: 4 concurrent jobs, no deadlock pass (10-run soak)
No crash-recovery data loss: kill mid-job, replay WAL, no partial summary visible 100%

20.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/consolidate/summarize_cluster.json 10 episodic records, temporal proximity 1 Semantic summary, 10 RETIRED
fixtures/consolidate/summarize_multi.json 30 records, 3 distinct clusters 3 Semantic summaries
fixtures/consolidate/reflect_basic.json 20 Semantic records >=1 reflection record
fixtures/consolidate/merge_near_dup.json 2 Semantic, cosine 0.95 1 canonical, 2 RETIRED
fixtures/consolidate/merge_distinct.json 2 Semantic, cosine 0.60 No merge, both retained
fixtures/consolidate/link_graph.json Multi-hop link structure Correct BFS neighbor set
fixtures/consolidate/retired_recall.json Recall after retire 0 results for retired IDs
fixtures/consolidate/crash_mid_summarize/ Kill after store write, before index update Replay: summary present, indices rebuilt
fixtures/consolidate/scheduler_count.json 51 records in ns Summarize job enqueued, metrics updated
fixtures/consolidate/concurrent_jobs.json 8 simultaneous jobs, pool=4 4 run, 4 queue, no deadlock

20.6 Pitfalls

  • Consolidation loop on its own output: a fresh summary can look "similar" to its now-RETIRED source, spawning a pointless merge job — exclude RETIRED from merge scans, tag records with consolidation_epoch to block same-epoch re-consolidation.
  • Oversized summary: a 50-record/500-token cluster can yield a 10k-token summary — cap max_summary_tokens, split into sub-clusters if exceeded.
  • Link graph ahead of store after crash: snapshot written before WAL-flushing link deltas — WAL-log every add_edge with an epoch, rebuild from WAL on startup like the record store.
  • Worker starvation: a large-window reflect job can hold a permit for minutes, starving summarize — time-box every job (job_timeout_ms), cancel+re-queue on timeout.
  • Retired records reappearing post-compaction: compaction must explicitly skip Status::Retired during index rebuild.
  • Reflection amplification: high-salience insights trigger cascading merge scans — rate-limit reflect enqueue to 1/namespace/reflect_interval.
  • Empty merge result (model failure): validate merged_content.len()>0; on empty, abort and keep both originals, consolidate_merge_empty_total.

20.7 Performance Concerns

  • Agglomerative clustering is O(N²): fine at N<=50 (~2500 comparisons), so cap max_cluster_size=50 and split oversized clusters by time bucket first.
  • HNSW merge-candidate scan per new Semantic record — throttle to once per record per merge_scan_interval under high ingest rate.
  • LinkGraph write-lock contention across concurrent summarize/merge jobs — batch edge updates, acquire the write lock once per job not per edge.
  • LlmIface summarize call (~500ms–2s per cluster) bounded by max_concurrent_jobs; reduce cluster size if P99 > 5s.
  • WAL append rate from consolidation (summary + N retires) is negligible vs ingest rate.
  • Embedding buffer pooled per job, released on completion (same 6 KB/embedding concern as ingest).
  • Deep/wide BFS on a 1M-node graph — cap max_traversal_depth=10, max_traversal_nodes=1000, return truncated=true on limit.

20.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty record_ids: ConsolidationError::EmptyJob, no LlmIface call/store write.
  • All-RETIRED namespace: reflect returns empty Vec (not an error).
  • Re-merging an already-merged canonical: allowed via derived_from chains, capped max_merge_depth=5.
  • Concurrent consolidation + forget(): missing/retired record mid-cluster is skipped, logged consolidate_skip_missing_record, rest proceeds.
  • Backward clock jump: track last-reflect by ULID epoch, not wall clock, to avoid immediate re-run.
  • Link-graph cycles: depth cap + visited set prevent infinite BFS loops.
  • Summary near-identical to a prior summary: merge pipeline catches and merges it (one extra round-trip, acceptable).
  • Crash after summary write but before all sources retired: idempotent re-run detects the existing summary via derived_from match, skips re-summarize, finishes retiring remaining Active sources.

20.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Containment Recovery
LlmIface summarize timeout consolidate_llm_timeout_total Job fails, no data loss Re-queue after backoff, sources stay Active Investigate latency, raise job_timeout_ms
LinkGraph snapshot corruption Checksum mismatch on load Traversal queries fail Rebuild from WAL delta, serve stale snapshot meanwhile mem-cli graph rebuild --ns <ns>
Worker pool exhaustion consolidate_worker_pool_wait_ms p99>1s Jobs queue indefinitely Alert, shed low-priority (Merge/Reflect) Raise max_concurrent_jobs or scale out
Retire-before-summary crash Replay finds RETIRED source, no summary Orphaned RETIRED records Idempotent replay re-runs summarize Monitor consolidate_orphan_repair_total
Malformed reflect JSON Deserialization error Zero reflections created WARN, skip malformed insight Inspect LlmIface output, fix prompt
Merge queue unbounded growth consolidate_merge_queue_depth spike OOM risk Hard cap (max_merge_queue=10k), drop oldest Drain queue, lower merge_sim_threshold

Postmortem: mem-cli consolidate audit --ns <ns> lists RETIRED records + derived_into chains, verifying none are orphaned.

20.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: consolidate_{summarize,reflections,merge}_total, consolidate_records_retired_total, consolidate_job_latency_ms, consolidate_worker_pool_utilization, consolidate_merge_queue_depth, consolidate_{llm,job}_timeout_total.
  • Tracing: consolidate.job{job_type,ns,record_count} with children cluster, llm_{summarize,reflect,merge}, store_write, index_update, link_graph_update.
  • Link graph persistence: snapshot every graph_snapshot_interval (5 min default) via atomic rename + WAL delta between snapshots; startup loads snapshot then replays deltas.
  • Config hot-reload: summarize_threshold/reflect_interval/merge_sim_threshold/max_concurrent_jobs via SIGHUP, without interrupting running jobs.
  • Deployment: background service; SIGTERM drains running jobs (max drain_timeout_ms) for zero-downtime deploy.
  • Migration: new EdgeKind variant bumps link-graph snapshot version; old snapshots rebuild from WAL (forward-compatible).
  • CLI: mem-cli consolidate status --ns <ns> (queue depth, last-run timestamps, RETIRED count, edge count, worker utilization); mem-cli consolidate run --job summarize --ns <ns> manual trigger; mem-cli consolidate audit --ns <ns> --status RETIRED for compliance.
  • Namespace isolation: all jobs scoped to NsId, no cross-namespace LlmIface calls or graph edges.

21. Phase 9 — Forgetting / decay / tiering — decay model, eviction policy (importance x recency x frequency), TTL, hot/warm/cold tiering, archival

21.1 Objectives

  • mem-forget: decay scoring, eviction scheduler, TTL enforcement, three-tier storage. Decay score is a composite of Salience, recency (last_access), access_count — multiplicative, not additive.
  • TTL: per-record Option<Duration>, eviction at expiry + next GC sweep. Tiering: hot (mmap resident, HNSW active) → warm (cold pages, index stale) → cold (WAL-only, index removed) → archived (external blob + tombstone).
  • Invariant: every eviction logged with EvictionReason, no silent removal. GC runs in a background tokio task, never blocking remember()/recall(). Tier transitions are crash-safe (partial transitions detectable on recovery).
  • Scope: mem-forget consumes mem-store/mem-index write handles; mem-telemetry taps all eviction events.

21.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P11 (No Silent Loss — every eviction emits EvictionReason WAL entry before removal), P5 (Bounded Resource Usage — DecayConfig.cold_threshold and archive_threshold bound each tier), P2 (Crash-Only/Replayability — TtlIndex persisted as WAL side-channel, replayed on startup), I2 (No-Silent-Loss — EvictionReason code required for every transition), I11 (TTL Expiry — expired records removed ≤ gc_interval + sweep_budget), ADR-10 (WAL eviction entries), ADR-03 (Tokio GC sweep task), PB1 (Park et al. 2023 — recency×importance composite decay inspired by retrieval score).

Decay model: r(t)=exp(-λ_r·Δt_hours) (recency), f(n)=log(1+n)/log(1+N_max) (frequency, n=access_count), i=salience.0 (importance); composite D(rec)=i·r(t)·f(n), lower = more evictable. DecayConfig{lambda_r,lambda_i_weight,freq_cap,gc_interval,cold_threshold,archive_threshold} per NsId. score() is pure; cached in DashMap<MemoryId,(f32,Timestamp)>, invalidated on access_count bump.

  • TTL (ttl.rs): TtlIndex: BTreeMap<Timestamp,Vec<MemoryId>>; remember() inserts (now+ttl,id) if set; GC drains expiry<=now entries → EvictionReason::TtlExpired; persisted as WAL side-channel (TtlEntry{id,expiry}), replayed on startup.
  • Eviction scheduler (eviction.rs): background task wakes on gc_interval or ForgetHandle::trigger_gc(); heap-pops lowest D(rec) until EvictionBudget{max_records,max_bytes} satisfied, skipping pinned (salience==1.0 or pin flag). Pipeline: score_all()→select_candidates()→tier_down_or_remove()→log_evictions(). EvictionReason∈{TtlExpired,DecayThreshold(f32),StoragePressure,ExplicitForget,ArchivalPolicy}.
  • Tiering (tier.rs): HOT --[score<cold_threshold]--> WARM --[score<archive_threshold]--> COLD --[policy]--> ARCHIVED, access promotes back up. TierRecord{id,tier,tier_changed_at} in mem-store metadata. Hot→Warm: remove from active HNSW, mark stale, MADV_COLD. Warm→Cold: remove BM25/recency/importance entries, keep WAL record. Cold→Archived: serialize to archive backend (file/S3), WAL tombstone ArchivedRecord{id,location}. Tier-up on access (D(rec)>warm_threshold): restore index entries, re-embed if stale.
  • Index cleanup: IndexCleanupHandle::remove(id) atomically clears HNSW/BM25/recency/importance under one IndexWriteGuard (no partial-index visibility); mem-store compaction updates TierRecord atomically with segment compaction.
  • Observability: counter forget.evictions_total{reason,kind,namespace}, histogram forget.decay_score, gauges forget.{hot,warm,cold}_count; structured log per eviction {id,kind,reason,decay_score,tier_before,tier_after,ns}.
  • Tests: TTL expiry (mock clock), decay eviction (1000 records, lowest-score removed), tier down/up + index restoration, no-silent-loss (eviction log vs store scan), archive roundtrip integrity.

21.3 Deliverable

  • mem-forget compiles against mem-store/mem-index; GC background task evicts by decay+TTL, logs all removals; tier transitions observable via telemetry gauges+structured logs.
  • mem-cli forget gc --namespace <ns> --dry-run previews candidates; mem-cli tier status reports hot/warm/cold counts + estimated storage per namespace.

21.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Target
TTL expiry latency (mock clock) <= 2× gc_interval after expiry
Eviction log completeness 100% — zero silent removals in test suite
GC background task p99 pause < 20 ms for 100K-record namespace
Tier-up on access latency < 5 ms (index re-entry + mmap warm)
Archive roundtrip correctness SHA-256 match on retrieve
test_no_silent_loss passes 100% of runs (no flakiness)
Decay score determinism identical input → identical score, bit-for-bit
mem-forget/tests/ all tests green in CI

21.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture file Purpose Expected output
fixtures/decay/uniform_salience.jsonl 1000 records, salience=0.5, varying last_access Bottom 10% have D < 0.1; evicted first
fixtures/ttl/short_ttl_batch.jsonl 50 records with ttl=1s All 50 in eviction log within 2× gc_interval
fixtures/tier/cold_access.jsonl Record in COLD tier accessed Tier record shows COLD→HOT; index entries restored
fixtures/tier/partial_transition_crash.binlog WAL truncated mid hot→warm On recovery: record stays HOT (rollback), no dangling index
fixtures/archive/roundtrip.jsonl 10 records archived, retrieved Content SHA matches; tombstone present in store
fixtures/regression/decay_score_v1.jsonl Known inputs + expected scores Bit-exact score match (regression guard)
fixtures/eviction/log_completeness.jsonl 500 records evicted across reasons Eviction log count == store delta

21.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Recovery
Decay cache staleness Recently-accessed records still evicted Flush DashMap score cache on every last_access update
Index/store split-brain post-crash Record in store but not HNSW (or vice versa) Compaction reconciles; IndexCleanupHandle::remove idempotent
TtlIndex not replayed on restart Expired records never removed WAL side-channel replay in mem-store init; recovery test
GC starved under write pressure Hot count grows unbounded (forget.gc_lag_seconds up) Dedicated GC thread outside tokio worker pool, priority boost
Tier-up thundering herd Mass warm/cold access spikes index.write_queue_depth Rate-limit/batch tier-up requests
Archive backend unavailable Cold→archived fails, cold count grows Retry+backoff, alert on sustained failure; records stay cold not lost
Pin flag bypass Pinned (salience=1.0) record evicted via explicit forget Assert pin in all eviction paths; explicit forget requires force=true

21.7 Performance Concerns

  • Decay scoring is O(N)/sweep (~50ms at 1M records) — skip records with last_access > now-gc_interval, batch in 4K chunks.
  • Eviction candidate selection is heap-pop O(N log k), k~1000 — avoid full sort.
  • TtlIndex ~24 MB at 1M records — shard by day bucket above 10M.
  • MADV_COLD is an advisory hint only, never relied on for correctness.
  • HNSW write lock during cleanup can block reads — read-preferring RwLock, GC yields after each removal.
  • Archive upload copies the record to a staging buffer before releasing any store lock (no lock held during blob upload).
  • Tier-up on the recall hot path budgeted <5 ms; over budget → return result, schedule tier-up async.

21.8 Edge Cases

  • Simultaneous TTL expiry + explicit forget(): idempotent, second sees tombstone, logs AlreadyEvicted.
  • created_at in the future (clock skew): decay score=1.0 (never evicted), TTL still computed from created_at, clock_skew_detected warning.
  • salience=0.0: evicted on first GC sweep — expected for ephemeral scratch records.
  • access_count overflow: saturates at u32::MAX, f(n)→1.0 (highly protected).
  • GC during compaction: GC acquires the store lock with a timeout, skips/reschedules the cycle rather than deadlocking.
  • Namespace deletion while GC running: cancellation token, GC drains and exits cleanly.
  • Archive URI collision: keyed by MemoryId (ULID), collision probability negligible.
  • Empty-namespace GC completes in O(1), no error.

21.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
GC task panics forget.gc_panics_total No eviction, storage grows Supervisor restarts with backoff Stateless restart-safe
Archive write failure forget.archive_errors_total, cold plateaus Records stuck cold Alert on threshold, disable archival Restore backend, replay pending queue
Decay score NaN/Inf debug_assert!(finite), forget.score_nan_total Wrong eviction order Clamp [0,1] in release Guard log(0) with +1 in formula
TTL replay failure Store has expired records outside TtlIndex Stale records persist Recovery scan re-enqueues all expired Fix side-channel, add recovery test
Tier table corruption index.dangling_refs gauge Stale/wrong-tier retrieval Consistency check on open Rebuild from WAL replay + reconciliation

21.10 Operational Notes

  • Alerts: forget.hot_count>budget_threshold (storage pressure); forget.gc_lag_seconds>2×gc_interval (GC starvation); sustained forget.archive_errors_total>0.
  • Tracing: one gc_sweep{ns,candidates,evicted,elapsed_ms} span per sweep.
  • Config hot-reload: DecayConfig reloaded from mem-store config table without restart.
  • Migration: new decay factor bumps decay_schema_version, invalidates cached scores on startup.
  • Rollback: gc_enabled=false per namespace halts eviction for offline investigation.
  • CLI: mem-cli forget gc --dry-run (safe candidate preview); mem-cli archive restore --id <MemoryId> (re-ingest, re-index, promote to hot).

22. Phase 10 — Re-ranking + MMR + filters — cross-encoder rerank interface, MMR diversity, metadata + time-travel filters

22.1 Objectives

  • Cross-encoder rerank hook in mem-retrieve: pluggable RerankIface (default pass-through, LlmIface-backed option). MMR diversity selection over the retrieved candidate set.
  • Metadata filter DSL (MetaFilter) + time-travel (as_of) query execution. Retrieval determinism guaranteed: fixed index state + query → identical ranked set.
  • Rerank is optional (adds latency); MMR always available (configurable λ_mmr). Filters push down pre-retrieval where possible, post-filter as fallback.
  • Scope: mem-retrieve extended, mem-index gains filter push-down; LlmIface used for rerank only under RerankMode::LlmBacked.

22.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P4 (Retrieval Determinism — fixed index + query → deterministic ranked set with stable sort), P5 (Bounded Resource Usage — top_k_before/after caps reranker candidate window), I4 (Retrieval Determinism — rerank timeout fallback preserves pre-rerank deterministic order), ADR-09 (LlmIface rerank), ADR-06 (AnnBackend filter push-down), PB5 (Carbonell & Goldstein 1998 — MMR λ_mmr diversity control), PB12 (Graefe 1994 — push-down as iterator composability).

  • Rerank (rerank.rs): trait RerankIface{ fn rerank(query,candidates,cfg)->BoxFuture<Result<Vec<RetrievalResult>,MemError>> }; RerankConfig{mode,top_k_before,top_k_after,timeout}; RerankMode∈{PassThrough,LlmBacked,ExternalHttp(url)}. LlmBackedReranker calls LlmIface::rerank(query,texts)->Vec<f32>, maps back to .score, keeps components. ExternalHttpReranker POSTs {query,passages}, parses {scores}. Timeout falls back to pre-rerank order (retrieve.rerank_timeout_total); sort desc, stable MemoryId tie-break.
  • MMR (mmr.rs): mmr_select(candidates,embeddings,k,lambda), greedy O(k×N): score_mmr(d)=λ·sim(query,d)-(1-λ)·max_sim(selected,d); cosine on pre-fetched embeddings (mem-embed cache, batch-loaded once, no per-iteration I/O); λ=0 pure diversity, λ=1 degenerates to top-k; ties by MemoryId, fixed-precision f32 (no non-deterministic FMA).
  • MetaFilter DSL (filter.rs+mem-index/src/filter_pushdown.rs): enum MetaFilter{Eq,Lt/Gt/Le/Ge,In,And,Or,Not,KindFilter,NsFilter}. Push-down via per-field B-tree secondary indices for Eq/In/range; cross-field Or falls back to post-filter (can_pushdown() heuristic decides). Post-filter evaluates on the RetrievalResult stream after ANN+BM25.
  • as_of (as_of.rs): Query.as_of restricts to created_at<=as_of, internally MetaFilter::Le("created_at",as_of) pushed to the temporal BTreeMap<Timestamp,MemoryId> index (..=as_of range scan). Invariant: never returns post-as_of records (fixture-verified).
  • Pipeline (pipeline.rs): pre-filter (push-down candidate set) → ANN+BM25+recency+importance → RRF fusion (Phase 4) → post-filter (remaining MetaFilter evaluation) → optional rerank → MMR → top-k. Fixed step order, MMR always after rerank; all-filtered-to-zero returns Ok(vec![]) + retrieve.empty_result_total; each stage traced with candidate count in/out.
  • Tests: rerank passthrough/LLM-mock/timeout-fallback; MMR λ=0 diversity and λ=1 degenerate-to-top-k; filter push-down (KindFilter); as_of exclusion; determinism (100 identical runs).

22.3 Deliverable

  • mem-retrieve (rerank+MMR+filter+as-of) compiles, passes tests; RerankIface injectable, PassThrough default; MetaFilter parsed from Query.filters with push-down for simple predicates; as_of returns historically consistent sets; pipeline stages traced with candidate counts.

22.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Target
Rerank timeout fallback Activated within timeout + 5 ms; pre-rerank order preserved
MMR diversity (λ=0) Mean pairwise cosine similarity of selected set < 0.4 on test corpus
Filter push-down coverage Eq, In, range, KindFilter push-down verified by query plan log
as_of correctness Zero records with created_at > as_of in any result
Retrieval determinism 100/100 identical results on same state + query
Rerank+MMR end-to-end latency (100 candidates, mock LLM) p99 < 30 ms
All mem-retrieve/tests/ green in CI

22.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected output
fixtures/rerank/mock_scores.json Query + 20 candidates + mock reranker scores Final order matches mock score ranking
fixtures/rerank/timeout.json Reranker configured to timeout Result = pre-rerank top-k, rerank_timeout_total = 1
fixtures/mmr/diverse_corpus.jsonl 50 candidates with known embeddings λ=0.5: selected 10 have diversity metric > 0.6
fixtures/filter/kind_semantic.jsonl Mixed-kind records; KindFilter(Semantic) Only Semantic records in result
fixtures/filter/as_of_query.jsonl Records at t=1,2,3; as_of=2 Only t=1,2 records returned
fixtures/filter/complex_or.jsonl Or(Eq(a,1), Eq(b,2)) — non-pushdown path Correct post-filter result; no push-down in query plan log
fixtures/regression/determinism_100.jsonl Fixed index state + query Bit-exact identical result every run
fixtures/malformed/invalid_filter.json MetaFilter with unknown field type MemError::InvalidFilter; no panic

22.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug signal Recovery
Reranker score NaN Results sorted in arbitrary order LlmIface returns NaN score for empty passage retrieve.rerank_nan_total counter; final order scrambled Validate scores before applying: if !s.is_finite() { fallback }
MMR embedding fetch blocking MMR stage stalls retrieval > 100 ms Embedding cache miss for all candidates embed.cache_miss_total spike during retrieve Warm embedding cache on ingest; prefetch during RRF stage
Post-filter eliminating all candidates Empty result when records exist MetaFilter too narrow; push-down not reducing index scan retrieve.post_filter_zero_total; high pre_filter_count in trace Log filter expression with zero-result warning; guide operator
as_of with future timestamp All records visible (no filter effect) as_of > current time; no records filtered Warn if as_of > now; retrieve.as_of_future_total Clamp as_of to now; document in API
Determinism broken by f32 fma Different results across CPU architectures Compiler auto-vectorization uses FMA instructions CI test on x86 + aarch64; bit-exact comparison fails Use #[target_feature(disable="fma")] for similarity; document precision
MMR O(k×N) too slow for large k Retrieval latency > 100 ms for k=50, N=1000 Quadratic inner loop retrieve.mmr_duration_ms histogram tail Cap N (candidates entering MMR) at 200 via top_k_before; increase retrieval pre-filter
RerankMode::ExternalHttp TLS failure Fallback to PassThrough silently Certificate expired; HTTP client returns error retrieve.rerank_error_total{mode=ExternalHttp} Alert on counter; operator rotates certificate

22.7 Performance Concerns

  • Embedding batch load for MMR: load all N candidate embeddings from mem-embed cache in one batch call; single lock acquire; avoid per-record lock.
  • MMR cosine similarity: SIMD-accelerated dot product via packed_simd or wide; f32×D for D=1536 → ~400 ns per pair; k=20, N=200 → ~1.6 ms total.
  • Reranker HTTP round-trip: ExternalHttp adds network RTT; always apply within timeout budget; use connection pool (reqwest) not fresh connection per query.
  • MetaFilter evaluation on large post-filter set: short-circuit And on first false; Or on first true; document field ordering recommendation (most selective first).
  • Push-down index scan: secondary index B-tree range scan is O(log N + M) where M = matching records; orders of magnitude faster than full post-filter scan for selective filters.
  • Pipeline stage allocation: each stage materializes a Vec<RetrievalResult>; for k=100, N=1000 — ~80 KB allocation per query; acceptable; use Vec::with_capacity(n).

22.8 Edge Cases

  • k=0 query: return Ok(vec![]) immediately; skip all pipeline stages.
  • all candidates filtered by MetaFilter: return Ok(vec![]), emit metric; do not error.
  • MMR with k > N: return all N candidates in relevance order (MMR degenerates); no out-of-bounds.
  • Reranker returns fewer scores than candidates: MemError::RerankLengthMismatch(expected, got); fall back to original order.
  • as_of = Timestamp::MIN: return empty result (no records before epoch); not an error.
  • MetaFilter on non-indexed field: push-down returns None; full post-filter scan; log warn filter.unindexed_field.
  • Concurrent reranker calls: RerankIface must be Send + Sync; multiple queries may call simultaneously; LlmIface connection pool handles concurrency.
  • Empty embedding for candidate (embedding_ref invalid): skip candidate in MMR embedding load; log warn; candidate included in result with MMR score = relevance only.

22.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
RerankIface panics retrieve.rerank_panics_total; query returns error Single query fails Catch unwind in rerank wrapper; fall back to pre-rerank Fix panic; no data loss
MetaFilter parse error MemError::InvalidFilter returned to caller Single query fails Validated at Query construction time Return error to agent; agent reconstructs query
Temporal index corruption as_of queries return wrong records Wrong context assembled; agent reasoning degraded mem-index consistency check on open; temporal_index_errors gauge Rebuild temporal index from WAL (all created_at fields)
Reranker hallucinated scores Silent wrong ordering Subtly wrong retrieval; hard to detect Compare reranker score distribution vs. raw similarity in telemetry Log both pre/post rerank scores; alert on high divergence rerank.score_divergence_p99
Push-down returning wrong candidate set Systematic retrieval errors for filtered queries All filtered queries affected Push-down correctness tests (test_filter_eq_pushdown) Disable push-down (filter.pushdown_enabled=false); use post-filter; fix index

22.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: retrieve.rerank_duration_ms{mode} histogram; retrieve.mmr_duration_ms histogram; retrieve.pre_filter_candidates, retrieve.post_filter_candidates, retrieve.final_k gauges per query.
  • Tracing: span per pipeline stage; candidates_in, candidates_out attributes enable funnel analysis.
  • Reranker hot-swap: RerankIface behind Arc<dyn RerankIface>; hot-reload via ForgetHandle-style config update without restart.
  • MMR λ tuning: exposed as per-query param and namespace default; operators A/B test λ values via telemetry diversity metric.
  • Filter index maintenance: secondary indices updated on every remember() and eviction; no separate rebuild step needed unless corruption detected.
  • Rollback: if reranker degrades quality (detected via rerank.score_divergence_p99), set RerankMode::PassThrough in config; takes effect immediately on next query.
  • Compatibility: MetaFilter DSL is versioned (filter_dsl_version in Query); old clients send unversioned queries → default DSL v1 parsing.

23. Phase 11 — Multi-session / multi-agent isolation — namespaces, per-agent/per-user isolation, sharing policy, concurrent access

23.1 Objectives

  • NsId-based namespace isolation: every MemoryRecord belongs to exactly one namespace; cross-namespace access requires explicit sharing policy. Namespace = unit of isolation for store, index, decay, quota.
  • SharingPolicy{readable_by,writable_by} per namespace, enforced at mem-api. Concurrent access via per-namespace RwLock over index handles; mem-store WAL serializes writes globally.
  • SessionId wraps Ulid, a logical grouping within a namespace; remember() tags Provenance.session.
  • Isolation must be cryptographically enforceable at API level (namespace token) for multi-tenant deployments, optional for single-agent use. Namespace creation/deletion is transactional, no partial state.
  • Scope: mem-api gains NamespaceManager; mem-store, mem-index, mem-forget all keyed by NsId.

23.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P7 (Namespace Isolation — NsId prefix on all WAL + store entries), P8 (Explicit Provenance — Provenance.session per-remember), I7 (Namespace Isolation — cross-ns query must return MemError::PermissionDenied), ADR-12 (DashMap for NamespaceManager concurrency), ADR-20 (dep policy: NsQuota enforced without additional crates), S9 (SemVer — mem-api major bump on NsId schema change).

  • Namespace model (namespace.rs): NamespaceDescriptor{id:NsId,owner:AgentId,created_at,quota:NsQuota,sharing:SharingPolicy,status:NsStatus∈{Active,Draining,Deleted}}; NsQuota{max_records,max_bytes,max_ttl}; SharingPolicy{readable_by,writable_by}. NsId(Ulid) prefixes all WAL/store entries; descriptor persisted in a dedicated namespace table, replayed on startup. NamespaceManager{descriptors:DashMap<NsId,NamespaceDescriptor>, ns_handles:DashMap<NsId,NsHandle>}; NsHandle bundles StoreHandle/IndexHandles/ForgetHandle/RetrieveHandles, created lazily and cached.
  • Auth (auth.rs): NsToken = HMAC-SHA256(NsId,secret_key), issued at creation. AuthPolicy{mode: AuthMode::None|Token}. Every public mem-api fn takes &NsToken; verify_token(). Cross-ns read requires target's token OR listed in readable_by plus a valid own token.
  • Concurrency (concurrency.rs): per-namespace RwLock<NsHandle> (concurrent readers, exclusive writer). mem-store WAL: single global Mutex<WalWriter>, all namespaces share one prefix-keyed WAL file, batching up to 64 entries/fsync (per-namespace WAL file rejected — amplifies fsync cost). Index handles are per-namespace DashMap shards with per-namespace RwLock<HnswGraph>; no cross-namespace lock ordering needed (never hold two ns locks at once).
  • Lifecycle (lifecycle.rs): create_namespace(owner,quota,sharing)->(NsId,NsToken) writes descriptor to WAL, idempotent by owner+created_at. delete_namespace(ns_id,token) transitions Draining → background evicts records/index/segments → Deleted + WAL tombstone. list_namespaces(owner_token) filtered to owned+readable. Crash mid-deletion: Draining resumes on recovery; tombstone blocks NsId re-creation.
  • Sessions (session.rs): SessionId(Ulid); open_session(ns_id,token)->SessionHandle (no WAL write); injected into Provenance.session on remember(). Session-scoped recall via Query.session_filter, pushed down against a secondary B-tree (NsId,SessionId)→Vec<MemoryId> updated on ingest.
  • Enforcement (enforce.rs): enforce_access(caller_ns,target_ns,mode,policy): Read ok if same ns or listed in readable_by; Write ok if same ns or listed in writable_by; else MemError::AccessDenied{caller,target,mode} (logged as structured event).
  • Quota (quota.rs): QuotaTracker{DashMap<NsId,(AtomicU64 record_count, AtomicU64 bytes)>}, updated atomically on remember/forget; pre-write check rejects with QuotaExceeded; counters persisted and rebuilt from store scan on startup.
  • Tests: ns isolation (AccessDenied cross-ns), sharing-read grant, 10-task concurrent writes (no loss), 5-reader/2-writer concurrent (no deadlock, 5s timeout), quota-exceeded at limit, session-scoped filter isolation, crash-mid-delete resume, tampered-token rejection.

23.3 Deliverable

  • mem-api (NamespaceManager, sessions, sharing, quota, token auth) compiles; concurrent agents read/write isolated namespaces with no data leakage; namespace create/delete transactional and crash-safe; quotas enforced and durable across restart; all mem-api/tests/ pass with no deadlock/race findings.

23.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Target
Cross-namespace isolation Zero test cases return records from wrong namespace
Concurrent write throughput 1000 remember() ops/s at 10 concurrent agents, single namespace, no data loss
Deadlock detection --nocapture + cargo test with RUST_DEADLOCK_DETECTION=1; zero deadlocks in 10K concurrent ops
Quota enforcement QuotaExceeded returned within 1 remember() of limit; no over-commit
Namespace delete crash recovery Resume deletion on restart; zero dangling records after 100 crash-delete cycles
Token auth Tampered token rejected 100% of attempts; valid token accepted 100%
All mem-api/tests/ green in CI

Fixture Purpose Expected output
